- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `autosdv_rc_sim/track.py`

```python
"""The graded straight: its length follows from the speed profile it must hold.

    L = sum (v_i^2 - v_{i-1}^2) / (2a) + sum v_i T + v_top^2 / (2d) + run-out

With a = 2.5, d = 2.5, T = 3 s and 0 -> 2 -> 6 -> 11 m/s: 115.4 m, 17.8 s
(docs/design/rc-sim-design.md, "The track"). The written map is one straight
lane, split into a lanelet per segment so each carries its speed limit, plus
a 1 m lead-in behind the start pose, a stop line, a flat point cloud for the
map loader, and track.yaml with the profile and the start and goal poses.

    rc_sim_make_track --speeds 2,6,11 --hold 3 --accel 2.5 --decel 2.5 [--out DIR]

Refused: speeds that do not rise, a speed the model cannot hold with 5 %
headroom, an acceleration beyond the rear grip or the motor. Warned: a
deceleration the rear brake cannot reach (stop_overshoot says how far past
the stop line that puts the car; the grader's stop error does not allow for
it).
"""
import argparse
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import List, Tuple
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

import yaml

from autosdv_rc_sim.detmath import sqrt
from autosdv_rc_sim.drivetrain import brake_decel, drive_force, grip_accel, road_load, top_speed
from autosdv_rc_sim.params import load_vehicle, VehicleParams

LANE_WIDTH = 1.0
LEAD_IN = 1.0
DEFAULT_TRACK_NAME = 'straight_2_6_11'


class ProfileError(ValueError):
    """A profile the model cannot drive."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Segment:
    kind: str     # accel | hold | brake | runout
    s0: float
    s1: float
    v0: float
    v1: float

    @property
    def length(self) -> float:
        return self.s1 - self.s0

    @property
    def duration(self) -> float:
        if self.kind == 'hold':
            return self.length / self.v0
        if self.kind in ('accel', 'brake'):
            return 2.0 * self.length / (self.v0 + self.v1)
        return 0.0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TrackProfile:
    speeds: Tuple[float, ...]
    hold: float
    accel: float
    decel: float
    runout: float
    engage_velocity: float
    segments: Tuple[Segment, ...]

    @property
    def stop_line_s(self) -> float:
        return self.segments[-2].s1

    @property
    def length(self) -> float:
        return self.segments[-1].s1

    def v_target(self, s: float) -> float:
        """The speed Autoware's planner commands at position s along the straight."""
        if s >= self.stop_line_s:
            return 0.0
        for seg in self.segments:
            if s < seg.s1:
                if seg.kind == 'hold':
                    return seg.v0
                if seg.kind == 'accel':
                    v = sqrt(seg.v0 * seg.v0 + 2.0 * self.accel * max(0.0, s - seg.s0))
                    return max(min(v, seg.v1), self.engage_velocity)
                return min(seg.v0, sqrt(2.0 * self.decel * (self.stop_line_s - s)))
        return 0.0

    def table(self) -> str:
        rows = ['| Segment | Length | Time |', '|---|---|---|']
        for seg in self.segments:
            if seg.kind == 'accel':
                name = f'accelerate {seg.v0:g} → {seg.v1:g} m/s'
            elif seg.kind == 'hold':
                name = f'hold {seg.v0:g} m/s'
            elif seg.kind == 'brake':
                name = f'brake {seg.v0:g} m/s → stop on the stop line'
            else:
                name = 'run-out beyond the stop line'
            time = '—' if seg.kind == 'runout' else f'{seg.duration:.1f} s'
            rows.append(f'| {name} | {seg.length:.1f} m | {time} |')
        total_t = sum(seg.duration for seg in self.segments)
        rows.append(f'| **total** | **{self.length:.1f} m** | **{total_t:.1f} s** |')
        return '\n'.join(rows)


def make_profile(speeds, hold: float = 3.0, accel: float = 2.5, decel: float = 2.5,
                 runout: float = 10.0, engage_velocity: float = 0.25) -> TrackProfile:
    speeds = tuple(float(v) for v in speeds)
    if not speeds:
        raise ProfileError('need at least one speed')
    previous = 0.0
    for v in speeds:
        if v <= previous:
            raise ProfileError(f'speeds must rise strictly from 0: {speeds}')
        previous = v
    if hold <= 0.0 or accel <= 0.0 or decel <= 0.0 or runout < 0.0:
        raise ProfileError('hold, accel and decel must be positive and runout not negative')
    segments, s, v0 = [], 0.0, 0.0
    for v in speeds:
        length = (v * v - v0 * v0) / (2.0 * accel)
        segments.append(Segment('accel', s, s + length, v0, v))
        s += length
        segments.append(Segment('hold', s, s + v * hold, v, v))
        s += v * hold
        v0 = v
    length = v0 * v0 / (2.0 * decel)
    segments.append(Segment('brake', s, s + length, v0, 0.0))
    s += length
    segments.append(Segment('runout', s, s + runout, 0.0, 0.0))
    return TrackProfile(speeds, float(hold), float(accel), float(decel), float(runout),
                        float(engage_velocity), tuple(segments))


def stop_overshoot(profile: TrackProfile, vehicle: VehicleParams, level: float = 1.0) -> float:
    """Metres past the stop line that physics forces: braking from the top speed at the
    profile's decel where the car can, at ESC brake `level` where it cannot."""
    v_top = profile.speeds[-1]
    n = 2000
    dv = v_top / n
    distance = 0.0
    for i in range(n):
        v = (i + 0.5) * dv
        distance += v * dv / min(profile.decel, brake_decel(vehicle, v, level))
    return max(0.0, distance - profile.segments[-2].length)


def check_feasible(profile: TrackProfile, vehicle: VehicleParams) -> List[str]:
    """Raise ProfileError for a profile the model cannot drive; return warnings."""
    top = top_speed(vehicle)
    for v in profile.speeds:
        if v > 0.95 * top:
            raise ProfileError(
                f'{v:g} m/s cannot be held: the model tops out at {top:.2f} m/s and a hold '
                f'needs 5 % headroom')
        need = vehicle.effective_mass * profile.accel * 1.1 + road_load(vehicle, v)
        if drive_force(vehicle, 1.0, v) < need:
            raise ProfileError(f'the motor cannot accelerate at {profile.accel:g} m/s^2 up to {v:g} m/s')
    if profile.accel > 0.9 * grip_accel(vehicle):
        raise ProfileError(
            f'{profile.accel:g} m/s^2 is beyond the rear grip (launch limit '
            f'{grip_accel(vehicle):.2f} m/s^2)')
    warnings = []
    overshoot = stop_overshoot(profile, vehicle)
    if overshoot > 0.05:
        warnings.append(
            f'the rear brake gives {brake_decel(vehicle, 1.0):.2f}-'
            f'{brake_decel(vehicle, profile.speeds[-1]):.2f} m/s^2, not {profile.decel:g}: '
            f'expect {overshoot:.1f} m past the stop line')
    return warnings


def _osm(profile: TrackProfile) -> str:
    half = LANE_WIDTH / 2.0
    # The lead-in is a lanelet of its own (limit: the first speed), then one per segment
    stations = [-LEAD_IN, 0.0] + [seg.s1 for seg in profile.segments]
    top = profile.speeds[-1]
    limits = [profile.speeds[0]] + [
        seg.v1 if seg.kind == 'accel' else (seg.v0 if seg.kind == 'hold' else top)
        for seg in profile.segments]
    root = ET.Element('osm', {'version': '0.6', 'generator': 'autosdv_rc_sim make-track'})
    ET.SubElement(root, 'MetaInfo', {'format_version': '1', 'map_version': '1'})
    next_id = [1]

    def new_id():
        next_id[0] += 1
        return next_id[0] - 1

    def node(x, y):
        nid = new_id()
        el = ET.SubElement(root, 'node', {'id': str(nid), 'lat': '0', 'lon': '0'})
        for k, v in (('local_x', f'{x:.4f}'), ('local_y', f'{y:.4f}'), ('ele', '0.0000')):
            ET.SubElement(el, 'tag', {'k': k, 'v': v})
        return nid

    def way(nodes, tags):
        wid = new_id()
        el = ET.SubElement(root, 'way', {'id': str(wid)})
        for n in nodes:
            ET.SubElement(el, 'nd', {'ref': str(n)})
        for k, v in tags:
            ET.SubElement(el, 'tag', {'k': k, 'v': v})
        return wid

    left = [node(s, half) for s in stations]
    right = [node(s, -half) for s in stations]
    stop = [node(profile.stop_line_s, half), node(profile.stop_line_s, -half)]
    line = (('type', 'line_thin'), ('subtype', 'solid'))
    bounds = [(way([left[i], left[i + 1]], line), way([right[i], right[i + 1]], line))
              for i in range(len(stations) - 1)]
    way(stop, (('type', 'stop_line'),))
    for (lw, rw), limit in zip(bounds, limits, strict=True):
        rel = ET.SubElement(root, 'relation', {'id': str(new_id())})
        ET.SubElement(rel, 'member', {'type': 'way', 'role': 'left', 'ref': str(lw)})
        ET.SubElement(rel, 'member', {'type': 'way', 'role': 'right', 'ref': str(rw)})
        for k, v in (('type', 'lanelet'), ('subtype', 'road'), ('location', 'urban'),
                     ('one_way', 'yes'), ('participant:vehicle', 'yes'),
                     ('speed_limit', f'{limit * 3.6:.1f}')):
            ET.SubElement(rel, 'tag', {'k': k, 'v': v})
    ET.indent(root)
    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' + ET.tostring(root, encoding='unicode') + '\n'


def _pcd(profile: TrackProfile) -> str:
    points = []
    n = int(round((profile.length + LEAD_IN) / 0.25))
    for i in range(n + 1):
        x = -LEAD_IN + 0.25 * i
        for y in (LANE_WIDTH / 2.0, -LANE_WIDTH / 2.0):
            points.append(f'{x:.3f} {y:.3f} 0.000')
    header = ['# .PCD v0.7 - Point Cloud Data file format', 'VERSION 0.7', 'FIELDS x y z',
              'SIZE 4 4 4', 'TYPE F F F', 'COUNT 1 1 1', f'WIDTH {len(points)}', 'HEIGHT 1',
              'VIEWPOINT 0 0 0 1 0 0 0', f'POINTS {len(points)}', 'DATA ascii']
    return '\n'.join(header + points) + '\n'


def _track_yaml(profile: TrackProfile) -> str:
    data = {
        'speeds': list(profile.speeds), 'hold': profile.hold, 'accel': profile.accel,
        'decel': profile.decel, 'runout': profile.runout,
        'engage_velocity': profile.engage_velocity, 'lead_in': LEAD_IN, 'lane_width': LANE_WIDTH,
        'start_pose': {'x': 0.0, 'y': 0.0, 'yaw': 0.0},
        'goal_pose': {'x': round(profile.stop_line_s, 4), 'y': 0.0, 'yaw': 0.0},
        'stop_line_s': round(profile.stop_line_s, 4), 'length': round(profile.length, 4),
    }
    return '# Generated by rc_sim_make_track; regenerate, do not edit.\n' + yaml.safe_dump(
        data, sort_keys=False, default_flow_style=None)


def write_track(profile: TrackProfile, out_dir) -> None:
    out = Path(out_dir)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out / 'lanelet2_map.osm').write_text(_osm(profile))
    (out / 'pointcloud_map.pcd').write_text(_pcd(profile))
    (out / 'map_projector_info.yaml').write_text('projector_type: local\n')
    (out / 'track.yaml').write_text(_track_yaml(profile))


def _read(directory) -> dict:
    with open(Path(directory) / 'track.yaml') as f:
        return yaml.safe_load(f)


def load_track(directory) -> TrackProfile:
    d = _read(directory)
    return make_profile(d['speeds'], d['hold'], d['accel'], d['decel'], d['runout'],
                        d['engage_velocity'])


def track_poses(directory) -> dict:
    d = _read(directory)
    return {'start_pose': d['start_pose'], 'goal_pose': d['goal_pose']}


def default_track_dir() -> Path:
    source = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'tracks' / DEFAULT_TRACK_NAME
    if source.is_dir():
        return source
    from ament_index_python.packages import get_package_share_directory
    return Path(get_package_share_directory('autosdv_rc_sim')) / 'tracks' / DEFAULT_TRACK_NAME


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Size and write the graded straight.')
    ap.add_argument('--speeds', default='2,6,11', help='speeds to hold [m/s], rising')
    ap.add_argument('--hold', type=float, default=3.0, help='hold time per speed [s]')
    ap.add_argument('--accel', type=float, default=2.5, help="the planner's acceleration [m/s^2]")
    ap.add_argument('--decel', type=float, default=2.5, help="the planner's deceleration [m/s^2]")
    ap.add_argument('--runout', type=float, default=10.0, help='beyond the stop line [m]')
    ap.add_argument('--variant', default='nominal', help='vehicle model variant to check against')
    ap.add_argument('--out', default='', help='output directory (default tmp/rc_sim/tracks/straight_<speeds>)')
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        speeds = [float(x) for x in args.speeds.split(',') if x.strip()]
        profile = make_profile(speeds, args.hold, args.accel, args.decel, args.runout)
        warnings = check_feasible(profile, load_vehicle(variant=args.variant))
    except (ProfileError, ValueError) as e:
        print(f'error: {e}', file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    print(profile.table())
    for w in warnings:
        print(f'warning: {w}', file=sys.stderr)
    out = args.out or 'tmp/rc_sim/tracks/straight_' + '_'.join(f'{v:g}' for v in profile.speeds)
    write_track(profile, out)
    print(f'wrote {out}: lanelet2_map.osm, pointcloud_map.pcd, map_projector_info.yaml, track.yaml')
    print(f'drive it: just sim rc track:={out}')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

python3 -m autosdv_rc_sim.track --speeds 2,6,11 --hold 3 --accel 2.5 --decel 2.5 --out tracks/straight_2_6_11
python3 -m pytest test/test_track.py -q

The map holds nine lanelets: the 1 m lead-in, then one per segment. (`python3 -m` puts the source package first, because cwd is on `sys.path`; the vehicle interface is not needed here.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the recipe** to `just/sim.just`, after the existing recipes, keeping them all:

```just
# The web UI address. Override for a shared host: RC_SIM_WEB_ADDR=127.0.0.1:8092 just sim rc
web_addr := env_var_or_default('RC_SIM_WEB_ADDR', '0.0.0.0:8081')

#   just sim make-track                                  2,6,11 m/s, 3 s holds, a 2.5, d 2.5
#   just sim make-track --speeds 2,6,11 --hold 3 --accel 2.5 --decel 2.5 --out tmp/rc_sim/tracks/mine
# Prints the segment table; refuses a profile the model cannot drive.
# Size and write a straight track (lanelet2 map, point cloud, track.yaml) from a speed profile
[positional-arguments]
make-track *ARGS:
    #!/usr/bin/env bash
    source scripts/env.sh

## Task 14: The lockstep harness `lockstep.py`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/autosdv_rc_sim/lockstep.py`
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/test/test_lockstep.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ActuatorCore`, `build_controller_params`, `normalize_params` (Task 4), `load_speed_controller`, `DEFAULT_SPEED_CONTROLLER`, `VelocityEstimator` (Task 3), `Plant` (Task 11), `TrackProfile` (Task 13), `detmath`.
- Produces:
  - `DT = 0.001`, `DT_NS`, `CONTROL_NS = 10_000_000`, `REPORT_NS = 50_000_000`, and `COLUMNS = ("t", "s", "y", "yaw", "target", "measured", "speed", "pwm", "steer_pwm", "mode", "kappa_r", "slip_r", "ax", "delta", "emergency", "phase")`.
  - `share_file(pkg, rel) -> Path`, `read_ros_params(path) -> dict`, `load_actuator_params(extra=None) -> dict`, `load_velocity_report_params() -> dict`.
  - `pursuit(x, y, yaw, v, wheel_base, max_angle) -> float`.
  - `ProfileScenario(profile)` and `EstopScenario(profile, resume_speed=6.0, resume_accel=2.5, resume_hold=3.0)`, each with `name`, `.command(t_ns, s, v) -> (target, emergency, phase)`, `.done(t_ns, s, v, target) -> bool`; `EstopScenario.trigger_s`.
  - `Trace(scenario, variant, rows)` with `.column(name)`, `.csv_text()`, `.digest()`, `.write_csv(path)`, `Trace.read_csv(path)`.
  - `Lockstep(vehicle, actuator_params, controller_overrides, profile, variant="nominal")` with `.run(scenario, max_time=90.0) -> Trace`.

Order within each 1 ms step at time t: a velocity report if t is on the 50 ms grid (`VelocityEstimator.report` → `ActuatorCore.on_velocity`); a control tick if t is on the 10 ms grid (scenario target and emergency, pursuit steering → `on_command` → `tick` → a trace row); then `Plant.step` with the PWM held, and every edge it returns → `VelocityEstimator.on_edge`. This is the loop plant → hall edges → speed estimate → shell + SpeedPID → tracker → plant. All times are integer nanoseconds, converted to seconds for the core (`t / 1e9`). The steering command goes through the actuator's own Ackermann/ratio code, so the plant sees the same steering PWM the car would.

"""The lockstep harness: the car's classes, driven on a simulated clock."""
from autosdv_rc_sim.lockstep import (
    EstopScenario, load_actuator_params, Lockstep, ProfileScenario)
from autosdv_rc_sim.params import load_vehicle
from autosdv_rc_sim.track import default_track_dir, load_track, stop_overshoot

PROFILE = load_track(default_track_dir())
OPEN = {'max_pwm_offset': 100, 'integral_limit': 100.0}
# A set that can follow the whole profile: the reference Task 16 finds by search
TUNED = dict(OPEN, kp_speed=7.0, ki_speed=43.0, kd_speed=1.5)


def run(overrides=None, scenario=None, variant='nominal'):
    vehicle = load_vehicle(variant=variant)
    ls = Lockstep(vehicle, load_actuator_params(), overrides or {}, PROFILE, variant)
    return ls.run(scenario or ProfileScenario(PROFILE))


def test_lockstep_uses_rc_sim_ratio():
    assert load_actuator_params()['tire_angle_to_steer_ratio'] == -143.0
    assert load_vehicle().tire_angle_to_steer_ratio == -143.0
    assert load_actuator_params()['speed_controller_dt_source'] == 'loop'


def test_production_coefficients_cannot_reach_11():

    assert max(trace.column('speed')) < 8.0          # 370 + 55 tops out near 6.4 m/s
    assert max(trace.column('pwm')) <= 425


def test_opened_limit_reaches_11_stays_in_lane_and_stops():
    trace = run(OPEN)
    assert max(trace.column('speed')) > 10.5
    assert max(abs(y) for y in trace.column('y')) < 0.2
    assert abs(trace.column('speed')[-1]) < 0.01
    assert 'active' in trace.column('mode') and 'brake' in trace.column('mode')


def test_a_tuned_controller_stops_at_the_line_with_no_allowance():

    assert stop_overshoot(PROFILE, load_vehicle()) == 0.0
    trace = run(TUNED)
    assert abs(trace.column('speed')[-1]) < 0.01
    assert abs(trace.column('s')[-1] - PROFILE.stop_line_s) <= 0.5


def test_emergency_stop_overrides_then_resumes():
    trace = run(OPEN, EstopScenario(PROFILE))
    phases = trace.column('phase')
    assert phases.index('run') < phases.index('estop') < phases.index('resume')
    modes = [m for m, ph in zip(trace.column('mode'), phases) if ph == 'estop']
    assert set(modes) <= {'override_brake', 'override_neutral'}
    assert min(trace.column('speed')) >= -0.05

    assert max(resumed) > 5.0

def test_csv_round_trip(tmp_path):

    trace.write_csv(tmp_path / 't.csv')

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `autosdv_rc_sim/lockstep.py`

```python
"""Deterministic lockstep simulation: the car's code against the plant, one process, no ROS.

    plant --edges--> VelocityEstimator --20 Hz--> ActuatorCore (shell + SpeedPID) --100 Hz--> PWM
      ^                                                ^ target, steering                      |
      |                                     scenario + pursuit tracker                         |
      +---------------------------- 1 kHz, PWM held between ticks -----------------------------+

VelocityEstimator and ActuatorCore are the classes velocity_report and the
actuator run on the car (autosdv_vehicle_interface). The actuator's
parameters come from the same files the launch loads: actuator.yaml,
vehicle_info.param.yaml and config/actuator_rc_sim.param.yaml, with
speed_controller_dt_source 'loop' as the Lab 2 launches use. No wall clock, no
messages, no libm (see detmath): a run is bit-identical on any machine.
"""
import csv
from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import io
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional

import yaml

from autosdv_vehicle_interface.actuator_core import (
    ActuatorCore, build_controller_params, normalize_params)
from autosdv_vehicle_interface.speed_controller import DEFAULT_SPEED_CONTROLLER, load_speed_controller
from autosdv_vehicle_interface.velocity_estimator import VelocityEstimator

from autosdv_rc_sim.detmath import atan, atan2, sin
from autosdv_rc_sim.params import VehicleParams
from autosdv_rc_sim.plant import Plant
from autosdv_rc_sim.track import TrackProfile

DT = 0.001
DT_NS = 1_000_000
CONTROL_NS = 10_000_000
REPORT_NS = 50_000_000
COLUMNS = ('t', 's', 'y', 'yaw', 'target', 'measured', 'speed', 'pwm', 'steer_pwm', 'mode',
           'kappa_r', 'slip_r', 'ax', 'delta', 'emergency', 'phase')
_INT_COLUMNS = {'pwm', 'steer_pwm', 'emergency'}
_STR_COLUMNS = {'mode', 'phase'}

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
_SOURCE_DIRS = {
    'autosdv_vehicle_interface': REPO / 'src/vehicle/autosdv_vehicle_launch/autosdv_vehicle_interface',
    'autosdv_vehicle_description': REPO / 'src/vehicle/autosdv_vehicle_launch/autosdv_vehicle_description',
    'autosdv_rc_sim': REPO / 'src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim',
}


def share_file(pkg: str, rel: str) -> Path:
    """A package's file: from this checkout if present, else from the installed share."""
    source = _SOURCE_DIRS[pkg] / rel
    if source.is_file():
        return source
    from ament_index_python.packages import get_package_share_directory
    return Path(get_package_share_directory(pkg)) / rel


def read_ros_params(path) -> dict:
    with open(path) as f:
        return dict(yaml.safe_load(f)['/**']['ros__parameters'])


def load_actuator_params(extra: Optional[dict] = None) -> dict:
    raw = {}
    raw.update(read_ros_params(share_file('autosdv_vehicle_interface', 'params/actuator.yaml')))
    raw.update(read_ros_params(share_file('autosdv_vehicle_description',
                                          'config/vehicle_info.param.yaml')))
    raw.update(read_ros_params(share_file('autosdv_rc_sim', 'config/actuator_rc_sim.param.yaml')))
    raw['speed_controller_dt_source'] = 'loop'
    raw.update(extra or {})
    return normalize_params(raw)


def load_velocity_report_params() -> dict:
    return read_ros_params(share_file('autosdv_vehicle_interface', 'params/velocity_report.yaml'))


class _QuietLogger:
    def _drop(self, *args, **kwargs):
        pass

    debug = info = warning = warn = error = fatal = _drop


def pursuit(x: float, y: float, yaw: float, v: float, wheel_base: float, max_angle: float) -> float:
    """Pure pursuit onto the straight's centre line (y = 0); stands in for Autoware's tracker."""
    lookahead = 1.0 + 0.3 * abs(v)
    alpha = atan2(-y, lookahead) - yaw
    delta = atan(2.0 * wheel_base * sin(alpha) / lookahead)
    return max(-max_angle, min(max_angle, delta))


class ProfileScenario:
    """The graded run: the straight's position-based profile to the stop line."""

    name = 'profile'

    def __init__(self, profile: TrackProfile, settle: float = 2.0):
        self.profile = profile
        self.settle_ns = int(round(settle * 1e9))
        self._still_since = None

    def command(self, t_ns: int, s: float, v: float):
        return self.profile.v_target(s), False, 'run'

    def done(self, t_ns: int, s: float, v: float, target: float) -> bool:
        if s > self.profile.stop_line_s - 2.0 and abs(v) < 0.01:
            if self._still_since is None:
                self._still_since = t_ns
            return t_ns - self._still_since >= self.settle_ns
        self._still_since = None
        return False


class EstopScenario:
    """The profile until mid top-speed hold; then target 0 with the gate's emergency; resume."""

    name = 'estop'

    def __init__(self, profile: TrackProfile, resume_speed: float = 6.0,
                 resume_accel: float = 2.5, resume_hold: float = 3.0):
        hold = [seg for seg in profile.segments if seg.kind == 'hold'][-1]
        self.profile = profile
        self.trigger_s = 0.5 * (hold.s0 + hold.s1)
        self.resume_speed = resume_speed
        self.resume_accel = resume_accel
        self.resume_ns = int(round((resume_speed / resume_accel + resume_hold) * 1e9))
        self.phase = 'run'
        self._still_since = None
        self._resume_at = None

    def command(self, t_ns: int, s: float, v: float):
        if self.phase == 'run':
            if s < self.trigger_s:
                return self.profile.v_target(s), False, 'run'
            self.phase = 'estop'
        if self.phase == 'estop':
            if abs(v) < 0.01:
                if self._still_since is None:
                    self._still_since = t_ns
                elif t_ns - self._still_since >= 1_000_000_000:
                    self.phase = 'resume'
                    self._resume_at = t_ns
            else:
                self._still_since = None
            if self.phase == 'estop':
                return 0.0, True, 'estop'
        elapsed = (t_ns - self._resume_at) / 1e9
        target = max(self.profile.engage_velocity,
                     min(self.resume_speed, self.resume_accel * elapsed))
        return target, False, 'resume'

    def done(self, t_ns: int, s: float, v: float, target: float) -> bool:
        return self.phase == 'resume' and t_ns - self._resume_at >= self.resume_ns


def _fmt(value) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return '1' if value else '0'
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return repr(value)
    return str(value)


@dataclass
class Trace:
    scenario: str
    variant: str
    rows: List[tuple]

    def column(self, name: str) -> list:
        i = COLUMNS.index(name)
        return [row[i] for row in self.rows]

    def csv_text(self) -> str:
        lines = [','.join(COLUMNS)]
        lines += [','.join(_fmt(v) for v in row) for row in self.rows]
        return '\n'.join(lines) + '\n'

    def digest(self) -> str:
        return hashlib.sha256(self.csv_text().encode()).hexdigest()

    def write_csv(self, path) -> None:
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        Path(path).write_text(self.csv_text())

    @staticmethod
    def read_csv(path, scenario: str = '', variant: str = '') -> 'Trace':
        reader = csv.reader(io.StringIO(Path(path).read_text()))
        header = next(reader)
        assert tuple(header) == COLUMNS, f'{path}: unexpected columns {header}'
        rows = []
        for raw in reader:
            row = []
            for name, text in zip(COLUMNS, raw):
                if name in _STR_COLUMNS:
                    row.append(text)
                elif name == 'emergency':
                    row.append(text == '1')
                elif name in _INT_COLUMNS:
                    row.append(int(text))
                else:
                    row.append(float(text))
            rows.append(tuple(row))
        return Trace(scenario, variant, rows)


class Lockstep:
    def __init__(self, vehicle: VehicleParams, actuator_params: dict, controller_overrides: dict,
                 profile: TrackProfile, variant: str = 'nominal'):
        self.vehicle = vehicle
        self.params = actuator_params
        self.overrides = dict(controller_overrides)
        self.profile = profile
        self.variant = variant
        vr = load_velocity_report_params()
        # velocity_report's own expression, so the result is bit-identical to the node's
        self.distance_per_marker = vr['wheel_diameter'] / 100.0 * 3.141592653589793 / vr['markers_per_rotation']

    def run(self, scenario, max_time: float = 90.0) -> Trace:
        p = self.params
        controller = load_speed_controller(
            DEFAULT_SPEED_CONTROLLER, build_controller_params(p, self.overrides))
        core = ActuatorCore(p, controller, logger=_QuietLogger(), start_time=0.0)
        estimator = VelocityEstimator(self.distance_per_marker)
        plant = Plant(self.vehicle, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        motor, steer = p['init_pwm'], p['init_steer']
        measured = 0.0
        target, emergency, phase = 0.0, False, 'run'
        rows = []
        n_max = int(round(max_time * 1e9)) // DT_NS
        for k in range(n_max):
            t = k * DT_NS
            now = t / 1e9
            x, y, yaw = plant.base_pose
            v = plant.state.vx
            if t % REPORT_NS == 0:
                measured = estimator.report(t)
                core.on_velocity(now, now, measured)
            if t % CONTROL_NS == 0:
                target, emergency, phase = scenario.command(t, x, v)
                core.gate_emergency = emergency
                angle = pursuit(x, y, yaw, v, p['wheelbase'], p['max_steering_angle'])
                core.on_command(now, target, angle)
                motor, steer = core.tick(now, now)
                rows.append((now, x, y, yaw, target, measured, v, motor, steer,
                             core.longitudinal.mode.value, plant.kappa_rear, plant.slip_rear,
                             plant.state.ax, plant.delta, emergency, phase))
                if scenario.done(t, x, v, target):
                    break
            for edge in plant.step(DT, motor, steer).edges:
                estimator.on_edge(edge)
        return Trace(scenario.name, self.variant, rows)
```

`3.141592653589793` is `math.pi`, written out so this module stays clear of `math` for the lint test in Task 17. velocity_report uses `math.pi`, the same double.

## Task 15: The grader `grade.py`, `just sim grade`, and the lab guide

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/autosdv_rc_sim/grade.py`
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/test/test_grade.py`
- Create: `docs/guides/rc-sim.md`
- Modify: `just/sim.just`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Lockstep`, `ProfileScenario`, `EstopScenario`, `Trace`, `COLUMNS`, `load_actuator_params` (Task 14), `load_track`, `default_track_dir`, `TrackProfile` (13), `load_vehicle`, `variant_names` (5).
- Produces:
  - `SUBMISSION_FILE = 'speed_controller_params.yaml'`, `ALLOWED_KEYS: Dict[str, type]`, `RANGES`, `SCENARIOS = ('profile', 'estop')`, `LIMITS: Dict[str, float]`, `PHYSICAL_LIMITS`, `PHYSICAL_CHECKS` (the check each physical limit decides), `LAUNCH_ACCEL_WINDOW = 0.05`, `SubmissionError(ValueError)`.
  - `load_submission(path) -> dict`.
  - `flow_mapping(params) -> str`.
  - `hold_metrics(trace, profile)`, `launch_metrics(trace, profile)`, `brake_metrics(trace, profile)`, `estop_metrics(trace, resume_speed=6.0)`.
  - `checks(metrics, limits=LIMITS) -> Dict[str, dict]`, giving 16 checks per variant (9 holds, launch slip and accel, stop error, brake lock, 3 e-stop), each `{'value', 'limit', 'pass'}`.
  - `run_one(job) -> Trace`.
  - `evaluate(params, variants=None, jobs=1, out_dir=None, track_dir=None, points=15.0, limits=LIMITS) -> dict`, with keys `score`, `marks`, `points`, `passed`, `total`, `variants`, `traces` (digests), `params`, `profile`.
  - `main(argv=None) -> int`.

Checks per variant (16; the limits below are the provisional ones, which Task 16 calibrates). Speeds are the true chassis speed; slip is the slip *velocity* at launch and κ only above 1 m/s (Review Focus 3):

| check | metric | passes when |
|---|---|---|
| `hold_rmse_2/6/11` | RMSE of speed − target from 1 s into each hold to its end | ≤ max(0.10, 0.05·v) m/s |
| `overshoot_2/6/11` | peak above the hold speed, % of the step | ≤ 10 % |
| `reach_2/6/11` | from the car entering the hold to speed ≥ v − 10 % of the step | ≤ 1.0 s |
| `launch_slip` | max rear slip velocity ωr − v until 1 s into the first hold | ≤ 0.5 m/s |
| `launch_accel` | max acceleration in that window, as the speed change over 50 ms (the per-millisecond `ax` carries contact impulses, not a lurch) | ≤ 4.0 m/s² |
| `stop_error` | \|final s − stop line\|, car at rest | ≤ 0.5 m (plain: d = 2.5 m/s² is within every variant's brake, so there is no allowance) |
| `brake_lock` | min rear κ in the braking segment while v > 1 m/s | ≥ −0.2 |
| `estop_no_reverse` | min speed over the e-stop run | ≥ −0.05 m/s |
| `estop_resume_overshoot` | peak above 6 m/s after the resume, % of 6 | ≤ 10 % |
| `estop_resume_slip` | max rear slip velocity in the first 1.5 s of the resume | ≤ 0.5 m/s |

Marks = points × passed / total over the 8 × 16 = 128 (variant, check) pairs, so each weighs the same and the marks stay out of 15. Task 16 calibrates the non-physical limits.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** — `test/test_grade.py`

```python
"""Submission loading, metrics on synthetic traces, and grading of known coefficient sets."""
import json

import pytest
import yaml

from autosdv_rc_sim import grade
from autosdv_rc_sim.lockstep import COLUMNS, load_actuator_params, Lockstep, ProfileScenario, Trace
from autosdv_rc_sim.params import load_vehicle
from autosdv_rc_sim.track import default_track_dir, load_track

PROFILE = load_track(default_track_dir())
OPEN = {'max_pwm_offset': 100, 'integral_limit': 100.0}
DEFAULTS = {'mode': 'active', 'phase': 'run', 'pwm': 370, 'steer_pwm': 489, 'emergency': False}


def synthetic(rows):
    return Trace('profile', 'nominal',
                 [tuple(r.get(c, DEFAULTS.get(c, 0.0)) for c in COLUMNS) for r in rows])


def write(tmp_path, data, sub=''):
    d = tmp_path / sub if sub else tmp_path
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (d / grade.SUBMISSION_FILE).write_text(yaml.safe_dump(data))
    return tmp_path


def test_loads_the_allowed_keys(tmp_path):
    params = grade.load_submission(write(tmp_path, {'kp_speed': 15, 'max_pwm_offset': 100}))
    assert params == {'kp_speed': 15.0, 'max_pwm_offset': 100}


def test_finds_config_subdirectory(tmp_path):
    assert grade.load_submission(write(tmp_path, {'ki_speed': 4.0}, 'config')) == {'ki_speed': 4.0}


@pytest.mark.parametrize('data, match', [
    ({'min_pwm': 350}, 'not tunable'),
    ({'max_pwm_offset': 150}, 'max_pwm_offset'),
    ({'max_pwm_offset': 80.5}, 'integer'),
    ({'kp_speed': '12'}, 'number'),
    ({'enable_conditional_integration': 1}, 'true or false'),
    ({'derivative_filter_alpha': 0.0}, 'derivative_filter_alpha'),
])
def test_refusals(tmp_path, data, match):
    with pytest.raises(grade.SubmissionError, match=match):
        grade.load_submission(write(tmp_path, data))


def test_missing_file(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(grade.SubmissionError, match=grade.SUBMISSION_FILE):
        grade.load_submission(tmp_path)


def test_flow_mapping():
    assert grade.flow_mapping({'kp_speed': 15.0, 'max_pwm_offset': 100}) == '{kp_speed: 15.0, max_pwm_offset: 100}'


def test_launch_slip_metric_finite_at_standstill():
    """Review Focus 3: a spinning wheel on a stopped car is a slip velocity, not an infinite ratio."""
    trace = synthetic([{'t': 0.01 * i, 's': 0.0, 'speed': 0.0, 'slip_r': 0.3, 'kappa_r': 2.0}
                       for i in range(100)])
    m = grade.launch_metrics(trace, PROFILE)
    assert m['slip'] == pytest.approx(0.3)


def test_a_perfect_follower_scores_full_hold_marks():
    rows, s, t = [], 0.0, 0.0
    while s < PROFILE.stop_line_s:
        v = PROFILE.v_target(s)
        rows.append({'t': t, 's': s, 'target': v, 'speed': v, 'measured': v})
        s += v * 0.01
        t += 0.01
    holds = grade.hold_metrics(synthetic(rows), PROFILE)
    for key in ('2', '6', '11'):
        assert holds[key]['rmse'] == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-9)
        assert holds[key]['overshoot_pct'] == 0.0
        assert holds[key]['reach_s'] == 0.0


def test_pwm_output_alpha_is_inert():
    """actuator.py reads pwm_output_alpha and never applies it (Spec Findings): two runs that
    differ only in that key leave identical traces. A key that does act (kp_speed) must not."""
    def run(**extra):
        ls = Lockstep(load_vehicle(), load_actuator_params(), dict(OPEN, **extra), PROFILE)
        return ls.run(ProfileScenario(PROFILE))
    a, b, other = run(pwm_output_alpha=0.25), run(pwm_output_alpha=1.0), run(kp_speed=20.0)
    assert a.rows == b.rows and a.digest() == b.digest()
    assert other.digest() != a.digest()


def test_production_defaults_lose_the_top_speed(tmp_path):
    report = grade.evaluate({}, variants=['nominal'], out_dir=tmp_path)
    c = report['variants']['nominal']['checks']
    assert not c['hold_rmse_11']['pass'] and not c['reach_11']['pass']
    assert report['total'] == 16
    marks = json.loads((tmp_path / 'marks.json').read_text())
    assert marks['marks'] == report['marks']
    assert (tmp_path / 'traces' / 'nominal_profile.csv').is_file()
    assert (tmp_path / 'traces' / 'nominal_estop.csv').is_file()


def test_print_params(tmp_path, capsys):
    assert grade.main([str(write(tmp_path, {'kp_speed': 15.0, 'max_pwm_offset': 100})), '--print-params']) == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out.strip() == '{kp_speed: 15.0, max_pwm_offset: 100}'


def test_bad_submission_exits_2(tmp_path, capsys):
    assert grade.main([str(write(tmp_path, {'min_pwm': 1}))]) == 2
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `autosdv_rc_sim/grade.py`

```python
"""Grade a Lab 2 Part 1 submission in the lockstep simulator: deterministic and headless.

    rc_sim_grade DIR                       every model variant, both scenarios; marks out of 15
    rc_sim_grade DIR --variant nominal     one variant (repeatable)
    rc_sim_grade DIR --jobs 4              variants in parallel (same result)
    rc_sim_grade DIR --print-params        the speed_controller_params mapping (for just sim rc)

DIR holds speed_controller_params.yaml (or config/speed_controller_params.yaml):
a flat mapping of the coefficients in ALLOWED_KEYS, and nothing else. The
coefficients go to the car's AckermannSpeedPID exactly as the actuator's
speed_controller_params would pass them. See docs/guides/rc-sim.md.
"""
import argparse
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import Dict

import yaml

from autosdv_rc_sim.detmath import sqrt
from autosdv_rc_sim.lockstep import (
    EstopScenario, load_actuator_params, Lockstep, ProfileScenario, Trace)
from autosdv_rc_sim.params import load_vehicle, variant_names
from autosdv_rc_sim.track import default_track_dir, load_track, TrackProfile

SUBMISSION_FILE = 'speed_controller_params.yaml'
ALLOWED_KEYS = {
    'kp_speed': float,
    'ki_speed': float,
    'kd_speed': float,
    'integral_limit': float,
    'enable_conditional_integration': bool,
    'derivative_filter_alpha': float,
    'pwm_output_alpha': float,   # read by the actuator but not applied (docs/guides/rc-sim.md)
    'max_pwm_offset': int,
}
# (low, high]: low exclusive for the alphas and the limits, inclusive elsewhere
RANGES = {
    'kp_speed': (0.0, 1000.0), 'ki_speed': (0.0, 1000.0), 'kd_speed': (0.0, 100.0),
    'integral_limit': (0.0, 1000.0), 'derivative_filter_alpha': (0.0, 1.0),
    'pwm_output_alpha': (0.0, 1.0), 'max_pwm_offset': (1, 100),
}
_EXCLUSIVE_LOW = {'integral_limit', 'derivative_filter_alpha', 'pwm_output_alpha'}
SCENARIOS = ('profile', 'estop')
RESUME_SPEED = 6.0
LIMITS = {
    'hold_rmse_rel': 0.05,         # m/s per m/s, floored at hold_rmse_floor
    'hold_rmse_floor': 0.10,       # m/s
    'overshoot_pct': 11.0,         # % of the step (calibrated: Task 16)
    'reach_s': 1.0,                # s
    'launch_slip': 0.5,            # m/s, physical
    'launch_accel': 4.8,           # m/s^2, over 50 ms (calibrated: Task 16)
    'stop_error': 0.5,             # m from the stop line, not calibrated
    'brake_kappa': -0.2,           # physical: past the tyre's peak
    'reverse_speed': -0.05,        # m/s, physical
    'resume_overshoot_pct': 10.0,  # % of the resume speed
    'resume_slip': 0.5,            # m/s, physical
}
PHYSICAL_LIMITS = ('launch_slip', 'brake_kappa', 'reverse_speed', 'resume_slip')
# The check each physical limit decides (calibrate.py refuses a reference that fails one)
PHYSICAL_CHECKS = {'launch_slip': 'launch_slip', 'brake_kappa': 'brake_lock',
                   'reverse_speed': 'estop_no_reverse', 'resume_slip': 'estop_resume_slip'}
# A lurch is the acceleration over this window, not the per-millisecond contact impulses
LAUNCH_ACCEL_WINDOW = 0.05  # [s]


class SubmissionError(ValueError):
    """A submission the grader refuses."""


def load_submission(path) -> dict:
    path = Path(path)
    if path.is_dir():
        for candidate in (path / SUBMISSION_FILE, path / 'config' / SUBMISSION_FILE):
            if candidate.is_file():
                path = candidate
                break
        else:
            raise SubmissionError(f'{path}: no {SUBMISSION_FILE} (or config/{SUBMISSION_FILE})')
    data = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text()) or {}
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise SubmissionError(f'{path}: expected a mapping of coefficients')
    unknown = sorted(set(data) - set(ALLOWED_KEYS))
    if unknown:
        raise SubmissionError(f'{path}: not tunable: {unknown}. Allowed: {sorted(ALLOWED_KEYS)}')
    params = {}
    for key, value in data.items():
        kind = ALLOWED_KEYS[key]
        if kind is bool:
            if not isinstance(value, bool):
                raise SubmissionError(f'{key} must be true or false, got {value!r}')
        elif kind is int:
            if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int):
                raise SubmissionError(f'{key} must be an integer, got {value!r}')
        else:
            if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(value):
                raise SubmissionError(f'{key} must be a finite number, got {value!r}')
            value = float(value)
        if key in RANGES:
            low, high = RANGES[key]
            too_low = value <= low if key in _EXCLUSIVE_LOW else value < low
            if too_low or value > high:
                raise SubmissionError(f'{key} = {value} is outside its range ({low}, {high}]')
        params[key] = value
    return params


def flow_mapping(params: dict) -> str:
    return '{' + ', '.join(f'{k}: {v}' for k, v in params.items()) + '}'


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- metrics

def _segments(profile: TrackProfile, kind: str):
    return [seg for seg in profile.segments if seg.kind == kind]


def hold_metrics(trace: Trace, profile: TrackProfile) -> Dict[str, dict]:
    t, s, v = trace.column('t'), trace.column('s'), trace.column('speed')
    out, previous = {}, 0.0
    for acc, hold in zip(_segments(profile, 'accel'), _segments(profile, 'hold')):
        speed = hold.v0
        key = f'{speed:g}'
        idx = [i for i, x in enumerate(s) if hold.s0 <= x < hold.s1]
        if not idx:
            out[key] = {'rmse': None, 'overshoot_pct': None, 'reach_s': None}
            previous = speed
            continue
        t0 = t[idx[0]]
        steady = [v[i] - speed for i in idx if t[i] >= t0 + 1.0]
        rmse = sqrt(sum(e * e for e in steady) / len(steady)) if steady else None
        overshoot = max(0.0, (max(v[i] for i in idx) - speed) / (speed - previous) * 100.0)
        level = speed - 0.1 * (speed - previous)
        start = next(i for i, x in enumerate(s) if x >= acc.s0)
        hit = next((i for i in range(start, len(t)) if v[i] >= level), None)
        out[key] = {'rmse': rmse, 'overshoot_pct': overshoot,
                    'reach_s': None if hit is None else max(0.0, t[hit] - t0)}
        previous = speed
    return out


def launch_metrics(trace: Trace, profile: TrackProfile) -> dict:
    t, s = trace.column('t'), trace.column('s')
    first_hold = _segments(profile, 'hold')[0]
    t_hold = next((ti for ti, x in zip(t, s) if x >= first_hold.s0), t[-1])
    window = [i for i, ti in enumerate(t) if ti <= t_hold + 1.0]
    slip, v = trace.column('slip_r'), trace.column('speed')
    # Mean acceleration over LAUNCH_ACCEL_WINDOW (a box filter on the true speed)
    lag = max(1, int(round(LAUNCH_ACCEL_WINDOW / (t[1] - t[0])))) if len(t) > 1 else 1
    accel = [(v[i] - v[i - lag]) / (t[i] - t[i - lag]) for i in window if i >= lag]
    return {'slip': max(slip[i] for i in window), 'accel': max(accel) if accel else 0.0}


def brake_metrics(trace: Trace, profile: TrackProfile) -> dict:
    s, v, kappa = trace.column('s'), trace.column('speed'), trace.column('kappa_r')
    brake = _segments(profile, 'brake')[0]
    kappas = [k for k, x, vi in zip(kappa, s, v) if x >= brake.s0 and vi > 1.0]
    return {'stop_error': s[-1] - profile.stop_line_s,
            'stopped': abs(v[-1]) < 0.01, 'min_kappa': min(kappas) if kappas else 0.0}


def estop_metrics(trace: Trace, resume_speed: float = RESUME_SPEED) -> dict:
    t, v, slip, phase = (trace.column('t'), trace.column('speed'), trace.column('slip_r'),
                         trace.column('phase'))
    resume = [i for i, ph in enumerate(phase) if ph == 'resume']
    if not resume:
        return {'min_speed': min(v), 'resume_overshoot_pct': None, 'resume_slip': None}
    t0 = t[resume[0]]
    peak = max(v[i] for i in resume)
    return {'min_speed': min(v),
            'resume_overshoot_pct': max(0.0, (peak - resume_speed) / resume_speed * 100.0),
            'resume_slip': max(slip[i] for i in resume if t[i] <= t0 + 1.5)}


def _upper(value, limit) -> dict:
    return {'value': value, 'limit': limit, 'pass': value is not None and value <= limit}


def _lower(value, limit) -> dict:
    return {'value': value, 'limit': limit, 'pass': value is not None and value >= limit}


def checks(metrics: dict, limits: Dict[str, float] = LIMITS) -> Dict[str, dict]:
    """The 16 checks of one variant: 9 holds, launch slip and accel, stop error, brake lock, 3 e-stop."""
    out = {}
    for key, m in metrics['holds'].items():
        rmse_limit = max(limits['hold_rmse_floor'], limits['hold_rmse_rel'] * float(key))
        out[f'hold_rmse_{key}'] = _upper(m['rmse'], rmse_limit)
        out[f'overshoot_{key}'] = _upper(m['overshoot_pct'], limits['overshoot_pct'])
        out[f'reach_{key}'] = _upper(m['reach_s'], limits['reach_s'])
    out['launch_slip'] = _upper(metrics['launch']['slip'], limits['launch_slip'])
    out['launch_accel'] = _upper(metrics['launch']['accel'], limits['launch_accel'])
    b = metrics['brake']
    out['stop_error'] = _upper(abs(b['stop_error']) if b['stopped'] else None, limits['stop_error'])
    out['brake_lock'] = _lower(b['min_kappa'], limits['brake_kappa'])
    e = metrics['estop']
    out['estop_no_reverse'] = _lower(e['min_speed'], limits['reverse_speed'])
    out['estop_resume_overshoot'] = _upper(e['resume_overshoot_pct'], limits['resume_overshoot_pct'])
    out['estop_resume_slip'] = _upper(e['resume_slip'], limits['resume_slip'])
    return out


def _round(x):
    if isinstance(x, float):
        return round(x, 6)
    if isinstance(x, dict):
        return {k: _round(v) for k, v in x.items()}
    return x


# --------------------------------------------------------------- grading

def run_one(job) -> Trace:
    variant, scenario, params, track_dir = job
    profile = load_track(track_dir)
    sc = ProfileScenario(profile) if scenario == 'profile' else EstopScenario(profile, RESUME_SPEED)
    return Lockstep(load_vehicle(variant=variant), load_actuator_params(), params, profile,
                    variant).run(sc)


def evaluate(params: dict, variants=None, jobs: int = 1, out_dir=None, track_dir=None,
             points: float = 15.0, limits: Dict[str, float] = LIMITS) -> dict:
    track_dir = str(track_dir or default_track_dir())
    variants = list(variants or variant_names())
    work = [(v, sc, dict(params), track_dir) for v in variants for sc in SCENARIOS]
    if jobs > 1:
        import multiprocessing
        with multiprocessing.get_context('spawn').Pool(jobs) as pool:
            traces = pool.map(run_one, work)
    else:
        traces = [run_one(job) for job in work]
    profile = load_track(track_dir)
    report = {'params': dict(params),
              'profile': {'speeds': list(profile.speeds), 'hold': profile.hold,
                          'accel': profile.accel, 'decel': profile.decel},
              'variants': {}, 'traces': {}}
    passed = total = 0
    by_key = {(tr.variant, tr.scenario): tr for tr in traces}
    for v in variants:
        prof_t, estop_t = by_key[(v, 'profile')], by_key[(v, 'estop')]
        metrics = {'holds': hold_metrics(prof_t, profile), 'launch': launch_metrics(prof_t, profile),
                   'brake': brake_metrics(prof_t, profile), 'estop': estop_metrics(estop_t)}
        result = checks(metrics, limits)
        report['variants'][v] = {'metrics': _round(metrics), 'checks': _round(result)}
        passed += sum(1 for c in result.values() if c['pass'])
        total += len(result)
        for tr in (prof_t, estop_t):
            report['traces'][f'{v}_{tr.scenario}'] = tr.digest()
            if out_dir:
                tr.write_csv(Path(out_dir) / 'traces' / f'{v}_{tr.scenario}.csv')
    report.update(passed=passed, total=total, points=points,
                  score=round(passed / total, 6), marks=round(points * passed / total, 2))
    if out_dir:
        Path(out_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (Path(out_dir) / 'marks.json').write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + '\n')
    return report


def _failures(result: Dict[str, dict]) -> str:
    out = []
    for name, c in result.items():
        if not c['pass']:
            value = 'none' if c['value'] is None else f"{c['value']:.3g}"
            out.append(f"{name} ({value} vs {c['limit']:.3g})")
    return ', '.join(out)


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Grade a speed-controller submission (Lab 2 Part 1).')
    ap.add_argument('submission', help=f'directory holding {SUBMISSION_FILE}, or the file')
    ap.add_argument('--variant', action='append', help='grade only this variant (repeatable)')
    ap.add_argument('--jobs', type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument('--out', default='', help='traces and marks.json (default tmp/rc_sim/grade/<name>)')
    ap.add_argument('--track', default='', help='track directory (default: the packaged straight)')
    ap.add_argument('--points', type=float, default=15.0)
    ap.add_argument('--print-params', action='store_true', help='print the mapping and exit')
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        params = load_submission(args.submission)
    except (SubmissionError, OSError, yaml.YAMLError) as e:
        print(f'error: {e}', file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if args.print_params:
        print(flow_mapping(params))
        return 0
    name = Path(args.submission).resolve().name
    out = Path(args.out or os.path.join('tmp', 'rc_sim', 'grade', name))
    report = evaluate(params, args.variant, args.jobs, out, args.track or None, args.points)
    print(f'rc_sim grade: {name} {flow_mapping(params)}')
    for v, r in report['variants'].items():
        n_pass = sum(1 for c in r['checks'].values() if c['pass'])
        print(f'  {v:<14} {n_pass:>2}/{len(r["checks"])}  {_failures(r["checks"])}')
    print(f"score {report['score']:.3f}  marks {report['marks']:.2f} / {report['points']:g}")
    print(f'traces and marks.json: {out}')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the guide** — `docs/guides/rc-sim.md`

````markdown
# The RC car simulator (Lab 2 Part 1)

The simulator runs the car's own vehicle interface — the safety shell and the
`AckermannSpeedPID` the actuator loads — against a model of the car fast
enough to matter: a motor that tops out at about 12 m/s, rear-wheel drive
that spins under hard throttle, tyres that slide. Design:
`docs/design/rc-sim-design.md`.

## Your task

Tune the car's speed controller. Nothing is written from scratch: study how
the textbook PID becomes `AckermannSpeedPID` (sampled integral, derivative
on the measurement with a low-pass filter, output clamp, integral limit,
conditional integration) in
`src/vehicle/autosdv_vehicle_launch/autosdv_vehicle_interface/autosdv_vehicle_interface/speed_controller.py`,
then set its coefficients. The deliverable is one file and a report.

`<your_dir>/speed_controller_params.yaml`, a flat mapping of these keys and
no others (anything else is refused):

| Key | Meaning | Production value |
|---|---|---|
| `kp_speed` | proportional gain [PWM counts per m/s] | 12.0 |
| `ki_speed` | integral gain | 10.0 |
| `kd_speed` | derivative gain, on the measured speed | 0.001 |
| `integral_limit` | the integral's clamp [counts] | 50.0 |
| `enable_conditional_integration` | freeze the integral while the output saturates | true |
| `derivative_filter_alpha` | the derivative's low-pass filter, (0, 1] | 0.15 |
| `pwm_output_alpha` | the actuator's output filter, (0, 1] — **read but not applied by today's actuator: it changes nothing** | 0.25 |
| `max_pwm_offset` | the output limit around neutral (370) [counts], 1–100 | 55 |

With the production values the controller tops out at PWM 425, about 6.4 m/s
on the model: it cannot hold 11 m/s. That is the first thing to find.

## How you are graded

Headless and deterministic: one Python process steps the car (1 kHz), the
hall sensor, `velocity_report`'s estimate, the actuator's shell and your
coefficients (100 Hz) on a simulated clock, so the same file gives the same
marks on any machine. The target is what Autoware's planner commands on the
graded straight (115.4 m): 0 → 2 → 6 → 11 m/s, 3 s holds, then a stop on
the stop line. Two runs per model variant:

- **profile** — speed holds at 2, 6 and 11 m/s (steady error, overshoot,
  time to reach), the launch from rest (no wheel spin, no lurch), the
  braking to the stop line (stop error, no wheel lock);
- **e-stop** — at 11 m/s the target drops to 0 with Autoware's emergency
  flag; the shell brakes; after the car has stopped it resumes to 6 m/s
  (no reversing, clean resume, no spin).

Eight model variants (mass, friction, battery voltage, motor constant, ESC
deadband) replace the per-car models, so coefficients tuned to one exact
model lose marks. Each of the 16 checks per variant is worth the same; marks
= 15 × passed / total over all 8 × 16 checks. The stop error is the distance
from the stop line, at most 0.5 m on every variant.

```bash
just sim grade ~/lab2/<student_id>_lab_2_1              # all variants (a minute or two)
just sim grade ~/lab2/<student_id>_lab_2_1 --variant nominal --jobs 4
```

Each line names the variant, how many checks passed, and each failure as
`check (value vs limit)`. Traces (100 Hz CSV: target, measured and true
speed, PWM, shell mode, slip) and `marks.json` are written to
`tmp/rc_sim/grade/<dir name>/`.
````

---

## Task 16: Calibrate the limits and record reference coefficients

The provisional limits in `LIMITS` come from the spec's intent, not from any controller. This task finds coefficients a competent student could reach, checks that every check can be passed on every variant, and sets the non-physical limits from that reference. It never ships a check nobody can pass.

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/testing/rc_sim/calibrate.py`
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/config/reference_speed_controller.yaml` (calibration output)
- Modify: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/autosdv_rc_sim/grade.py` (the `LIMITS` values only)
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/test/test_calibration.py`
- Modify: `docs/guides/rc-sim.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `grade.evaluate`, `grade.LIMITS`, `grade.PHYSICAL_LIMITS`, `grade.PHYSICAL_CHECKS`.
- Produces: `config/reference_speed_controller.yaml` (a valid submission file, `speed_controller_params` keys only). `calibrate.py [--jobs N]` prints the chosen coefficients, a worst-case table and a `LIMITS = {...}` block. It exits 0 only when the reference passes all 16 checks on all 8 variants with every value at most 90 % of its limit, 3 if the reference fails a physical check anywhere, and 4 if it passes but with less margin than that.

Rule for the limits. For each calibrated metric (`hold_rmse_rel`, `overshoot_pct`, `reach_s`, `launch_accel`, `resume_overshoot_pct`), the new limit is max(provisional limit, 1.5 × the reference's worst value across all variants), rounded up to 2 significant figures. The stop-error limit (a plain 0.5 m) and the physical limits (`launch_slip`, `brake_kappa`, `reverse_speed`, `resume_slip`, which come from `grade.PHYSICAL_LIMITS`) are never moved. They have no 1.5 × room, so their margin has to come from the coefficients: the search keeps the set that passes the most checks and, among those, the one whose worst stop-error or physical check sits furthest inside its limit. If the reference fails a physical check, the model or a variant is wrong for this profile: stop and report to the user with the numbers, rather than loosening it. A zero is a real value (a perfect overshoot of 0 % is not "missing"); only `None`, a metric the run never reached, counts as infinitely bad.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the calibration script** — `scripts/testing/rc_sim/calibrate.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Find reference coefficients for the rc_sim grader, and suggest its limits from them.

    python3 scripts/testing/rc_sim/calibrate.py --jobs 8

1. Grid-search the coefficients, every variant graded under the provisional
   limits; keep the set that passes most checks and, among those, the one whose
   worst stop-error or physical check sits furthest inside its limit (the
   smallest value/limit). Those limits never move, so their margin is the one
   that has to come from the coefficients.
2. If the winner fails a physical check anywhere, exit 3: the model or a
   variant cannot be driven on this profile, and no limit should be loosened
   to hide that.
3. Print, for each calibrated metric, the worst value across variants and the
   suggested limit = max(provisional, 1.5 x worst), 2 significant figures up.
   The stop error and the physical limits are not calibrated.
4. Regrade under the suggested limits; exit 4 if any check sits within 10 % of
   its limit (value/limit above MARGIN), exit 0 otherwise.
5. Write src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/config/reference_speed_controller.yaml.
"""
import argparse
import itertools
import math
import multiprocessing
from pathlib import Path
import sys

import yaml

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
for p in (REPO / 'src/vehicle/autosdv_vehicle_launch/autosdv_vehicle_interface', REPO / 'src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim'):
    sys.path.insert(0, str(p))

from autosdv_rc_sim import grade  # noqa: E402

GRID = {'kp_speed': (5.0, 6.0, 7.0), 'ki_speed': (42.0, 43.0, 44.0, 45.0), 'kd_speed': (1.25, 1.5, 1.75),
        'integral_limit': (100.0,)}
FIXED = {'max_pwm_offset': 100, 'enable_conditional_integration': True,
         'derivative_filter_alpha': 0.15}
OUT = REPO / 'src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/config/reference_speed_controller.yaml'
MARGIN = 0.9      # every check of the reference within 90 % of its limit
UNCALIBRATED = ('stop_error',) + tuple(grade.PHYSICAL_CHECKS.values())   # their limits do not move


def candidates():
    keys = list(GRID)
    for values in itertools.product(*(GRID[k] for k in keys)):
        yield dict(FIXED, **dict(zip(keys, values)))


def real(x):
    """A metric as a number; None (not measured: the run never got there) is infinitely bad."""
    return math.inf if x is None else x


def ratio(check):
    """value / limit: below 1 passes, for upper and lower limits alike (both lower limits are <= 0)."""
    if check['value'] is None:
        return math.inf
    return check['value'] / check['limit']


def margin_of(report, names=None):
    """The largest value/limit over the variants, for the named checks (default: all)."""
    return max(ratio(c) for r in report['variants'].values() for n, c in r['checks'].items()
               if names is None or n in names)


def score(params):
    """(checks passed, minus the worst value/limit among the checks whose limits do not move)."""
    report = grade.evaluate(params)
    return report['passed'], -margin_of(report, UNCALIBRATED), params


def up2(x):
    if x <= 0.0:
        return x
    exponent = math.floor(math.log10(x)) - 1
    return float(f'{math.ceil(x / 10 ** exponent) * 10 ** exponent:.2g}')


def worst(report):
    """Worst value per calibrated metric across variants."""
    out = {}
    for v in report['variants'].values():
        c = v['checks']
        speeds = [k[len('hold_rmse_'):] for k in c if k.startswith('hold_rmse_')]
        values = {
            'hold_rmse_rel': max(real(c[f'hold_rmse_{s}']['value']) / float(s) for s in speeds),
            'overshoot_pct': max(real(c[f'overshoot_{s}']['value']) for s in speeds),
            'reach_s': max(real(c[f'reach_{s}']['value']) for s in speeds),
            'launch_accel': real(c['launch_accel']['value']),
            'resume_overshoot_pct': real(c['estop_resume_overshoot']['value']),
        }
        for k, x in values.items():
            out[k] = max(out.get(k, -math.inf), x)
    return out


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--jobs', type=int, default=multiprocessing.cpu_count())
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    with multiprocessing.get_context('spawn').Pool(args.jobs) as pool:
        scored = pool.map(score, list(candidates()))
    passed, neg_margin, best = max(scored, key=lambda r: (r[0], r[1]))
    print(f'reference ({passed} checks passed over all variants; worst value/limit among the '
          f'uncalibrated checks {-neg_margin:.2f}): {grade.flow_mapping(best)}')
    report = grade.evaluate(best, jobs=args.jobs)
    physical_failures = [(v, name) for v, r in report['variants'].items()
                         for name in grade.PHYSICAL_CHECKS.values() if not r['checks'][name]['pass']]
    if physical_failures:
        print(f'STOP: the reference fails physical checks {physical_failures}; '
              'fix the model or the variant (and tell the user), do not loosen these.')
        return 3
    print('metric                 worst     provisional  suggested')
    suggested = dict(grade.LIMITS)
    for key, value in worst(report).items():
        suggested[key] = max(grade.LIMITS[key], up2(1.5 * value))
        print(f'{key:<22} {value:>9.4f} {grade.LIMITS[key]:>12.4g} {suggested[key]:>10.4g}')
    for key in grade.PHYSICAL_LIMITS:
        assert suggested[key] == grade.LIMITS[key]          # physical limits are never moved
    stop = max(real(r['checks']['stop_error']['value']) for r in report['variants'].values())
    print(f"stop_error (not calibrated)  worst {stop:.4f}  limit {grade.LIMITS['stop_error']:g}")
    print('LIMITS = ' + repr(suggested))
    final = grade.evaluate(best, jobs=args.jobs, limits=suggested)
    print(f"under the suggested limits: {final['passed']}/{final['total']} checks, "
          f'worst value/limit {margin_of(final):.2f} (must stay under {MARGIN:g})')
    if final['passed'] != final['total'] or margin_of(final) > MARGIN:
        print('STOP: the reference has too little margin under the suggested limits; '
              'the model, the variants or the search grid need work.')
        return 4
    OUT.write_text('# Reference coefficients for the rc_sim grader (scripts/testing/rc_sim/calibrate.py).\n'
                   + yaml.safe_dump(best, sort_keys=True))
    print(f'wrote {OUT}')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

(`math.log10` and `**` are fine here: this script is not on the lockstep path.)

## Task 17: Determinism — stress test, golden digests, the libm lint

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/test/test_determinism.py`
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/test/golden/nominal_digests.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `grade.evaluate`, `grade.load_submission`, the reference file (Task 16).
- Produces: tests, and the golden file `{"<variant>_<scenario>": "<sha256>", …}` for the nominal reference run.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests** — `test/test_determinism.py`

```python
"""Grading is bit-identical: under CPU load, across runs, and (golden file) across machines."""
import ast
import json
import multiprocessing
import os
from pathlib import Path

from conftest import PKG, VEHICLE_INTERFACE

from autosdv_rc_sim import grade

REFERENCE = PKG / 'config' / 'reference_speed_controller.yaml'
GOLDEN = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / 'golden' / 'nominal_digests.json'

LOCKSTEP_MODULES = [PKG / 'autosdv_rc_sim' / f for f in (
    'params.py', 'esc.py', 'drivetrain.py', 'tires.py', 'wheels.py', 'servo.py', 'hall.py',
    'chassis.py', 'plant.py', 'track.py', 'lockstep.py', 'grade.py')] + [
    VEHICLE_INTERFACE / 'autosdv_vehicle_interface' / f for f in (
        'actuator_core.py', 'longitudinal.py', 'speed_controller.py', 'safety.py',
        'velocity_estimator.py')]
FORBIDDEN_MATH = {'sin', 'cos', 'tan', 'asin', 'acos', 'atan', 'atan2', 'exp', 'expm1', 'log',
                  'log1p', 'log2', 'log10', 'pow', 'sinh', 'cosh', 'tanh', 'hypot', 'erf', 'gamma'}
# The one documented exception (Global Constraints, Review Focus 4): ActuatorCore.run_steering_control is
# production code, moved verbatim from the actuator. It calls math.tan/atan on the steering command, and
# its result is truncated to an integer PWM; the straight track commands about zero steering, and the
# golden digests and the CPU-stress test below catch a last-bit difference. Nothing else is allowed.
ALLOWED_MATH = {'actuator_core.py': {'tan', 'atan'}}


def offences(path):
    tree = ast.parse(path.read_text())
    found = []
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp) and isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow):
            ints = all(isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and type(n.value) is int
                       for n in (node.left, node.right))
            if not ints:
                found.append(f'{path.name}:{node.lineno}: ** (libm pow)')
        if (isinstance(node, ast.Attribute) and isinstance(node.value, ast.Name)
                and node.value.id == 'math' and node.attr in FORBIDDEN_MATH
                and node.attr not in ALLOWED_MATH.get(path.name, ())):
            found.append(f'{path.name}:{node.lineno}: math.{node.attr}')
        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module == 'math':
            bad = [a.name for a in node.names
                   if a.name in FORBIDDEN_MATH and a.name not in ALLOWED_MATH.get(path.name, ())]
            if bad:
                found.append(f'{path.name}:{node.lineno}: from math import {bad}')
        if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
            names = [a.name for a in node.names]
            if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module:
                names.append(node.module)
            if any(n.split('.')[0] in ('numpy', 'random') for n in names):
                found.append(f'{path.name}:{node.lineno}: imports numpy/random')
    return found


def test_lockstep_path_is_free_of_libm():
    found = [o for path in LOCKSTEP_MODULES for o in offences(path)]
    assert not found, 'non-deterministic calls on the lockstep path:\n' + '\n'.join(found)


def _busy(stop):
    x = 0
    while not stop.is_set():
        x += 1


def test_bit_identical_under_cpu_load(tmp_path):
    params = grade.load_submission(REFERENCE)
    quiet = grade.evaluate(params, variants=['nominal'], out_dir=tmp_path / 'quiet')
    ctx = multiprocessing.get_context('spawn')
    stop = ctx.Event()
    hogs = [ctx.Process(target=_busy, args=(stop,)) for _ in range(os.cpu_count() or 2)]
    for h in hogs:
        h.start()
    try:
        loaded = grade.evaluate(params, variants=['nominal'], jobs=2, out_dir=tmp_path / 'loaded')
    finally:
        stop.set()
        for h in hogs:
            h.join()
    assert loaded == quiet
    for name in ('nominal_profile.csv', 'nominal_estop.csv'):
        assert (tmp_path / 'quiet/traces' / name).read_bytes() == (tmp_path / 'loaded/traces' / name).read_bytes()


def test_golden_digests():
    """Any machine, any CPU: the reference run's traces hash to the committed digests.

    After an intended model change: RC_SIM_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 python3 -m pytest test/test_determinism.py
    and commit the new file with the change that caused it.
    """
    digests = grade.evaluate(grade.load_submission(REFERENCE), variants=['nominal'])['traces']
    if os.environ.get('RC_SIM_UPDATE_GOLDEN') == '1':
        GOLDEN.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        GOLDEN.write_text(json.dumps(digests, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + '\n')
    assert digests == json.loads(GOLDEN.read_text())


def test_the_allow_list_is_the_steering_code_and_nothing_wider():
    """The exception covers math.tan/atan in actuator_core.py, and only there."""
    assert ALLOWED_MATH == {'actuator_core.py': {'tan', 'atan'}}
    source = (VEHICLE_INTERFACE / 'autosdv_vehicle_interface' / 'actuator_core.py').read_text()
    used = {node.attr for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(source))
            if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute) and isinstance(node.value, ast.Name)
            and node.value.id == 'math' and node.attr in FORBIDDEN_MATH}
    assert used == {'tan', 'atan'}                      # the allow-list is exactly what the file uses
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Plant` (11), `ImuModel` (10), `load_vehicle` (5), `track_poses`, `default_track_dir` (13); the vehicle interface's `hardware:=sim` (Tasks 2–4) through `vehicle_interface.launch.xml`; `sim_pose_init` (autosdv_coach_lab); `quat_multiply`, `quat_conjugate`, `quat_rotate` (`autosdv_coach_lab.bench_plant`).
- Produces:
  - Node `rc_sim_plant` (`RcSimPlant(**kwargs)`), with parameters `vehicle_file`, `variant`, `track_dir`, `real_time_factor` (1.0), `clock_period` (0.005 s), `imu_rate` (100), `odom_rate` (50), `imu_topic`, `imu_frame`, `base_frame`, `map_frame`, `angular_velocity_offset_{x,y,z}` and `seed`.
  - Topics: in `/rc_sim/pwm` and `/initialpose3d`; out `/clock`, `/rc_sim/hall_edges`, `/sensing/camera/zedxm/imu/data`, `/localization/kinematic_state`, `/localization/acceleration`, TF `map→base_link`, and `/rc_sim/state` (`Float64MultiArray [t, x, y, yaw, vx, vy, yaw_rate, omega_f, omega_r, delta, kappa_r, duty, brake]`).
  - Launch `rc_sim.launch.xml` with arguments `track_dir`, `variant`, `speed_controller_params`, `real_time_factor`, `rviz`, `vehicle_model`, `sensor_model`, `vehicle_id`, `data_path`.
  - `smoke.py [--track DIR] [--timeout S] [--resources CSV] [--taskset CPUS] [launch_args…]`: exit 0 on PASS.

`/clock` is published every 5 ms of simulated time. That divides the actuator's 10 ms period and the 50 ms report period, and sends a tenth of the messages a 1 kHz clock would to every node. Task 20 measures the cost.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing node test** — `test/test_plant_node.py`

```python
"""rc_sim_plant: owns /clock, takes PWM, emits edges and state. Needs rclpy.

Run under ROS_DOMAIN_ID=73 and flock tmp/bench.lock.
"""
import os
import time

import pytest

os.environ.setdefault('ROS_DOMAIN_ID', '73')
rclpy = pytest.importorskip('rclpy')

from rclpy.executors import SingleThreadedExecutor  # noqa: E402
from rclpy.parameter import Parameter  # noqa: E402
from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock  # noqa: E402
from std_msgs.msg import Float64MultiArray, Int32MultiArray, Int64MultiArray  # noqa: E402

from autosdv_rc_sim.plant_node import RcSimPlant  # noqa: E402
from autosdv_vehicle_interface.hal import EDGE_TOPIC, PWM_TOPIC  # noqa: E402


@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def ros():
    rclpy.init()
    yield
    rclpy.try_shutdown()


def test_refuses_sim_time(ros):
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='owns /clock'):
        RcSimPlant(parameter_overrides=[Parameter('use_sim_time', value=True)])


def test_drives_on_pwm_and_publishes_clock_edges_and_state(ros):
    plant = RcSimPlant()
    probe = rclpy.create_node('rc_sim_probe')
    got = {'clock': [], 'edges': 0, 'state': None}
    probe.create_subscription(Clock, '/clock', lambda m: got['clock'].append(
        m.clock.sec + m.clock.nanosec * 1e-9), 100)
    probe.create_subscription(Int64MultiArray, EDGE_TOPIC,
                              lambda m: got.__setitem__('edges', got['edges'] + len(m.data)), 100)
    probe.create_subscription(Float64MultiArray, '/rc_sim/state',
                              lambda m: got.__setitem__('state', list(m.data)), 10)
    pwm = probe.create_publisher(Int32MultiArray, PWM_TOPIC, 10)
    executor = SingleThreadedExecutor()
    executor.add_node(plant)
    executor.add_node(probe)
    try:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 6.0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            pwm.publish(Int32MultiArray(data=[0, 420]))
            executor.spin_once(timeout_sec=0.01)
        assert got['clock'] and got['clock'][-1] > 2.0           # simulated time advanced
        assert got['clock'] == sorted(got['clock'])
        assert got['state'] is not None and got['state'][4] > 1.0   # vx
        assert got['edges'] > 20
    finally:
        plant.stop()
        probe.destroy_node()
        plant.destroy_node()
```
