- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `autosdv_rc_sim/plant_node.py`

```python
"""rc_sim_plant: the car's hardware for the interactive simulator (`just sim rc`).

  in   /rc_sim/pwm            Int32MultiArray [channel, count], from the actuator's SimPCA9685
       /initialpose3d         PoseWithCovarianceStamped: place the car (the planning
                              simulator's pose initializer publishes it)
  out  /clock                 every clock_period of simulated time
       /rc_sim/hall_edges     Int64MultiArray of edge times [ns], to velocity_report's SimGPIO
       /sensing/camera/zedxm/imu/data   raw IMU in zedxm_imu_link, carrying the static
                              offsets imu_corrector removes
       /localization/kinematic_state    Odometry map -> base_link: the true pose
       /localization/acceleration       AccelWithCovarianceStamped
       TF map -> base_link
       /rc_sim/state          [t, x, y, yaw, vx, vy, yaw_rate, omega_f, omega_r, delta,
                              kappa_r, duty, brake]

Physics runs at 1 kHz in its own thread, paced to real_time_factor x wall
time; when the CPU falls behind, simulated time slows instead of jumping.
This node owns /clock, so it runs with use_sim_time false and every other
node with true. PWM is held between writes, as the PCA9685 holds it.
"""
import threading
import time

from autosdv_coach_lab.bench_plant import quat_conjugate, quat_multiply, quat_rotate
from builtin_interfaces.msg import Time as TimeMsg
from geometry_msgs.msg import AccelWithCovarianceStamped, PoseWithCovarianceStamped, TransformStamped
from nav_msgs.msg import Odometry
import rclpy
from rclpy.executors import ExternalShutdownException, MultiThreadedExecutor
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, ReliabilityPolicy
from rclpy.time import Time
from rosgraph_msgs.msg import Clock
from sensor_msgs.msg import Imu
from std_msgs.msg import Float64MultiArray, Int32MultiArray, Int64MultiArray
import tf2_ros

from autosdv_rc_sim.detmath import atan2, cos, sin
from autosdv_rc_sim.imu import ImuModel
from autosdv_rc_sim.params import GRAVITY, load_vehicle
from autosdv_rc_sim.plant import Plant
from autosdv_rc_sim.track import default_track_dir, track_poses
from autosdv_vehicle_interface.hal import EDGE_TOPIC, PWM_TOPIC   # the one definition of the two topic names


def stamp(t_ns: int) -> TimeMsg:
    msg = TimeMsg()
    msg.sec = t_ns // 1_000_000_000
    msg.nanosec = t_ns % 1_000_000_000
    return msg


def quat_yaw(yaw: float):
    return (0.0, 0.0, sin(0.5 * yaw), cos(0.5 * yaw))


class RcSimPlant(Node):
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        super().__init__('rc_sim_plant', **kwargs)
        if self.get_parameter('use_sim_time').value:
            raise RuntimeError('rc_sim_plant owns /clock: run it with use_sim_time:=false')
        for name, default in (('vehicle_file', ''), ('variant', 'nominal'), ('track_dir', ''),
                              ('real_time_factor', 1.0), ('clock_period', 0.005),
                              ('imu_rate', 100.0), ('odom_rate', 50.0),
                              ('imu_topic', '/sensing/camera/zedxm/imu/data'),
                              ('imu_frame', 'zedxm_imu_link'), ('base_frame', 'base_link'),
                              ('map_frame', 'map'), ('angular_velocity_offset_x', 0.0),
                              ('angular_velocity_offset_y', 0.0), ('angular_velocity_offset_z', 0.0),
                              ('seed', 0)):
            self.declare_parameter(name, default)
        gp = lambda k: self.get_parameter(k).value  # noqa: E731
        self.p = load_vehicle(gp('vehicle_file') or None, gp('variant'))
        start = track_poses(gp('track_dir') or default_track_dir())['start_pose']
        self.plant = Plant(self.p, start['x'], start['y'], start['yaw'])
        self.imu = ImuModel(self.p, int(gp('seed')))
        self.gyro_offset = tuple(float(gp(f'angular_velocity_offset_{a}')) for a in 'xyz')
        self.base_frame, self.map_frame, self.imu_frame = gp('base_frame'), gp('map_frame'), gp('imu_frame')
        self.dt = self.p.physics_dt
        self.steps_clock = max(1, int(round(gp('clock_period') / self.dt)))
        self.steps_imu = max(1, int(round(1.0 / gp('imu_rate') / self.dt)))
        self.steps_odom = max(1, int(round(1.0 / gp('odom_rate') / self.dt)))
        self.wall_per_batch = self.steps_clock * self.dt / gp('real_time_factor')

        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.pwm = [self.p.init_pwm, self.p.init_steer]
        self.pending_pose = None
        reliable = QoSProfile(depth=100, reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.RELIABLE)
        self.create_subscription(Int32MultiArray, PWM_TOPIC, self.on_pwm, reliable)
        self.create_subscription(PoseWithCovarianceStamped, '/initialpose3d', self.on_initial_pose, 1)
        self.pub_clock = self.create_publisher(Clock, '/clock', 10)
        self.pub_edges = self.create_publisher(Int64MultiArray, EDGE_TOPIC, reliable)
        self.pub_imu = self.create_publisher(Imu, gp('imu_topic'), 10)
        self.pub_odom = self.create_publisher(Odometry, '/localization/kinematic_state', 10)
        self.pub_accel = self.create_publisher(AccelWithCovarianceStamped, '/localization/acceleration', 10)
        self.pub_state = self.create_publisher(Float64MultiArray, '/rc_sim/state', 10)
        self.tf = tf2_ros.TransformBroadcaster(self)
        self.tf_buffer = tf2_ros.Buffer()
        self.tf_listener = tf2_ros.TransformListener(self.tf_buffer, self)
        self.q_base_imu = None

        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.run, name='rc_sim_physics', daemon=True)
        self.thread.start()
        self.get_logger().info(
            f"rc_sim plant: variant {gp('variant')}, start {start}, /clock every "
            f'{self.steps_clock * self.dt * 1e3:.0f} ms, real-time factor {gp("real_time_factor")}')

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        self.thread.join(timeout=2.0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ input
    def on_pwm(self, msg) -> None:
        if len(msg.data) >= 2 and msg.data[0] in (0, 1):
            with self.lock:
                self.pwm[msg.data[0]] = int(msg.data[1])

    def on_initial_pose(self, msg) -> None:
        q = msg.pose.pose.orientation
        yaw = atan2(2.0 * (q.w * q.z + q.x * q.y), 1.0 - 2.0 * (q.y * q.y + q.z * q.z))
        with self.lock:
            self.pending_pose = (msg.pose.pose.position.x, msg.pose.pose.position.y, yaw)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ physics
    def run(self) -> None:
        edges, k = [], 0
        next_wall = time.monotonic()
        while not self._stop.is_set() and rclpy.ok():
            with self.lock:
                motor, steer = self.pwm
                pose, self.pending_pose = self.pending_pose, None
            if pose is not None:
                self.plant.reset_pose(*pose)
            for _ in range(self.steps_clock):
                edges.extend(self.plant.step(self.dt, motor, steer).edges)
                k += 1
                if k % self.steps_imu == 0:
                    self.publish_imu()
                if k % self.steps_odom == 0:
                    self.publish_odometry()
            if edges:
                self.pub_edges.publish(Int64MultiArray(data=edges))
                edges = []
            clock = Clock()
            clock.clock = stamp(self.plant.t_ns)
            self.pub_clock.publish(clock)
            next_wall += self.wall_per_batch
            delay = next_wall - time.monotonic()
            if delay > 0.0:
                time.sleep(delay)
            elif delay < -0.1:
                next_wall = time.monotonic()    # behind: let simulated time slow down

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ outputs
    def lookup_imu_mount(self) -> bool:
        if self.q_base_imu is not None:
            return True
        try:
            t = self.tf_buffer.lookup_transform(self.base_frame, self.imu_frame, Time())
        except (tf2_ros.LookupException, tf2_ros.ConnectivityException, tf2_ros.ExtrapolationException):
            return False
        r = t.transform.rotation
        self.q_base_imu = (r.x, r.y, r.z, r.w)
        return True

    def publish_imu(self) -> None:
        if not self.lookup_imu_mount():
            return
        s = self.plant.state
        gz, ax, ay = self.imu.sample(s)
        q_inv = quat_conjugate(self.q_base_imu)
        wx, wy, wz = quat_rotate(q_inv, (0.0, 0.0, gz))
        lx, ly, lz = quat_rotate(q_inv, (ax, ay, GRAVITY))
        qx, qy, qz, qw = quat_multiply(quat_yaw(s.yaw), self.q_base_imu)
        msg = Imu()
        msg.header.stamp = stamp(self.plant.t_ns)
        msg.header.frame_id = self.imu_frame
        msg.orientation.x, msg.orientation.y, msg.orientation.z, msg.orientation.w = qx, qy, qz, qw
        msg.angular_velocity.x = wx + self.gyro_offset[0]
        msg.angular_velocity.y = wy + self.gyro_offset[1]
        msg.angular_velocity.z = wz + self.gyro_offset[2]
        msg.linear_acceleration.x, msg.linear_acceleration.y, msg.linear_acceleration.z = lx, ly, lz
        g = max(self.p.gyro_noise_std, 1e-4) ** 2
        a = max(self.p.accel_noise_std, 1e-3) ** 2
        msg.angular_velocity_covariance = [g, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, g, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, g]
        msg.linear_acceleration_covariance = [a, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, a, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, a]
        msg.orientation_covariance = [1e-3, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1e-3, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1e-3]
        self.pub_imu.publish(msg)

    def publish_odometry(self) -> None:
        s = self.plant.state
        x, y, yaw = self.plant.base_pose
        vx, vy, wz = self.plant.base_twist
        t = stamp(self.plant.t_ns)
        qx, qy, qz, qw = quat_yaw(yaw)
        odom = Odometry()
        odom.header.stamp, odom.header.frame_id = t, self.map_frame
        odom.child_frame_id = self.base_frame
        odom.pose.pose.position.x, odom.pose.pose.position.y = x, y
        odom.pose.pose.orientation.z, odom.pose.pose.orientation.w = qz, qw
        odom.twist.twist.linear.x, odom.twist.twist.linear.y, odom.twist.twist.angular.z = vx, vy, wz
        for i in (0, 7, 14, 21, 28, 35):
            odom.pose.covariance[i] = 1e-4
            odom.twist.covariance[i] = 1e-4
        self.pub_odom.publish(odom)
        accel = AccelWithCovarianceStamped()
        accel.header.stamp, accel.header.frame_id = t, self.base_frame
        accel.accel.accel.linear.x, accel.accel.accel.linear.y = s.ax, s.ay
        for i in (0, 7, 14, 21, 28, 35):
            accel.accel.covariance[i] = 1e-3
        self.pub_accel.publish(accel)
        tf = TransformStamped()
        tf.header.stamp, tf.header.frame_id = t, self.map_frame
        tf.child_frame_id = self.base_frame
        tf.transform.translation.x, tf.transform.translation.y = x, y
        tf.transform.rotation.z, tf.transform.rotation.w = qz, qw
        self.tf.sendTransform(tf)
        esc = self.plant.esc_out
        self.pub_state.publish(Float64MultiArray(data=[
            self.plant.t_ns * 1e-9, x, y, yaw, s.vx, s.vy, s.yaw_rate, s.omega_f, s.omega_r,
            self.plant.delta, self.plant.kappa_rear, esc.duty, esc.brake]))


def main(args=None):
    rclpy.init(args=args)
    node = None
    try:
        node = RcSimPlant()
        executor = MultiThreadedExecutor(num_threads=2)
        executor.add_node(node)
        executor.spin()
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, ExternalShutdownException):
        pass
    finally:
        if node is not None:
            node.stop()
            node.destroy_node()
        rclpy.try_shutdown()
```

(`** 2` on two covariance constants is fine here: this module is not on the lockstep path, and the lint in Task 17 does not scan it.)

- [ ] **Step 5: The launch file** — `launch/rc_sim.launch.xml`

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!--
  The RC car simulator, interactive (`just sim rc`): Autoware in planning-simulator
  mode, the REAL vehicle interface with hardware:=sim, and the rc_sim plant, all on
  the plant's /clock. Design: docs/design/rc-sim-design.md.

    Autoware: map, planning, control, system, API, RViz (no sensing, localization, perception)
      /control/command/control_cmd -> actuator (SpeedPID, safety shell) -> /rc_sim/pwm -> rc_sim_plant
      rc_sim_plant -> /rc_sim/hall_edges -> velocity_report -> /vehicle/status/velocity_status
      rc_sim_plant -> /localization/kinematic_state, /localization/acceleration, TF map -> base_link
      rc_sim_plant -> /sensing/camera/zedxm/imu/data -> imu_corrector -> /sensing/imu/imu_data

  planning_simulator.launch.xml cannot drop simple_planning_simulator (only
  scenario_simulation does), so this file composes autoware.launch.xml with
  tier4_simulator_component.launch.xml and launch_dummy_vehicle:=false.
  Results here are not graded: separate processes on a simulated clock still race
  within a tick. `just sim grade` is the grade.
-->
<launch>
  <arg name="track_dir" default="$(find-pkg-share autosdv_rc_sim)/tracks/straight_2_6_11"
       description="a directory written by rc_sim_make_track (the map and track.yaml)"/>
  <arg name="variant" default="nominal" description="vehicle model variant (vehicle.yaml)"/>
  <arg name="speed_controller_params" default=""
       description="the coefficients as a YAML mapping, e.g. {kp_speed: 15.0, max_pwm_offset: 100}"/>
  <arg name="real_time_factor" default="1.0"/>
  <arg name="rviz" default="true"/>
  <arg name="vehicle_model" default="autosdv_vehicle"/>
  <arg name="sensor_model" default="autosdv_sensor_kit"/>
  <arg name="vehicle_id" default="$(env VEHICLE_ID default)"/>
  <arg name="data_path" default="$(env AUTOSDV_DATA_PATH ./data/autoware_data)"/>

  <set_parameter name="use_sim_time" value="true"/>

  <include file="$(find-pkg-share autoware_launch)/launch/autoware.launch.xml">
    <arg name="map_path" value="$(var track_dir)"/>
    <arg name="vehicle_model" value="$(var vehicle_model)"/>
    <arg name="sensor_model" value="$(var sensor_model)"/>
    <arg name="data_path" value="$(var data_path)"/>
    <arg name="launch_sensing" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_localization" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_perception" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_vehicle_interface" value="false"/>
    <arg name="system_run_mode" value="planning_simulation"/>
    <arg name="launch_system_monitor" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_dummy_diag_publisher" value="true"/>
    <arg name="diagnostic_graph_aggregator_graph_path"
         value="$(find-pkg-share autoware_launch)/config/system/diagnostics/autoware-main.yaml"/>
    <arg name="is_simulation" value="true"/>
    <arg name="use_sim_time" value="true"/>
    <arg name="rviz" value="$(var rviz)"/>
  </include>

  <!-- pose initializer (localization_sim_mode api) and dummy perception; no vehicle simulator -->
  <include file="$(find-pkg-share autoware_launch)/launch/components/tier4_simulator_component.launch.xml">
    <arg name="launch_dummy_perception" value="true"/>
    <arg name="launch_dummy_vehicle" value="false"/>
    <arg name="localization_sim_mode" value="api"/>
    <arg name="launch_dummy_doors" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_scenario_simulator_v2_adapter" value="false"/>
    <arg name="perception/enable_detection_failure" value="false"/>
    <arg name="perception/enable_object_recognition" value="false"/>
    <arg name="perception/enable_traffic_light" value="false"/>
    <arg name="perception/use_base_link_z" value="true"/>
    <arg name="sensing/visible_range" value="300.0"/>
    <arg name="vehicle_model" value="$(var vehicle_model)"/>
    <arg name="initial_engage_state" value="true"/>
    <arg name="vehicle_info_param_file"
         value="$(find-pkg-share autosdv_vehicle_description)/config/vehicle_info.param.yaml"/>
    <arg name="raw_vehicle_cmd_converter_param_path"
         value="$(find-pkg-share autoware_launch)/config/vehicle/raw_vehicle_cmd_converter/raw_vehicle_cmd_converter.param.yaml"/>
  </include>

  <!-- the car's vehicle interface, unchanged, on the simulated hardware -->
  <include file="$(find-pkg-share autosdv_vehicle_launch)/launch/vehicle_interface.launch.xml">
    <arg name="hardware" value="sim"/>
    <arg name="is_simulation" value="false"/>
    <arg name="vehicle_id" value="$(var vehicle_id)"/>
    <arg name="speed_controller_params" value="$(var speed_controller_params)"/>
    <arg name="speed_controller_dt_source" value="loop"/>
    <arg name="actuator_param_overrides"
         value="$(find-pkg-share autosdv_rc_sim)/config/actuator_rc_sim.param.yaml"/>
  </include>

  <!-- the plant owns /clock: the only node on wall time -->
  <group scoped="true">
    <set_parameter name="use_sim_time" value="false"/>
    <node pkg="autosdv_rc_sim" exec="rc_sim_plant" name="rc_sim_plant" output="screen">
      <param from="$(find-pkg-share individual_params)/config/$(var vehicle_id)/autosdv_sensor_kit/imu_corrector.param.yaml"/>
      <param name="use_sim_time" value="false"/>
      <param name="track_dir" value="$(var track_dir)"/>
      <param name="variant" value="$(var variant)"/>
      <param name="real_time_factor" value="$(var real_time_factor)"/>
    </node>
  </group>

  <include file="$(find-pkg-share autoware_imu_corrector)/launch/imu_corrector.launch.xml">
    <arg name="input_topic" value="/sensing/camera/zedxm/imu/data"/>
    <arg name="output_topic" value="/sensing/imu/imu_data"/>
    <arg name="param_file"
         value="$(find-pkg-share individual_params)/config/$(var vehicle_id)/autosdv_sensor_kit/imu_corrector.param.yaml"/>
  </include>

  <!-- the track's start pose (generated tracks start at the origin, facing +x) -->
  <node pkg="autosdv_coach_lab" exec="sim_pose_init" name="sim_pose_init" output="screen">
    <param name="x" value="0.0"/>
    <param name="y" value="0.0"/>
    <param name="yaw" value="0.0"/>
  </node>
</launch>
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The smoke test** — `scripts/testing/rc_sim/smoke.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Smoke test of the interactive RC simulator: Autoware drives the straight and stops.

    ROS_DOMAIN_ID=73 flock tmp/bench.lock python3 scripts/testing/rc_sim/smoke.py
    ... --resources tmp/rc_sim_budget.csv --taskset 0-3        the laptop budget (Task 20)
    ... speed_controller_params:='{max_pwm_offset: 100, integral_limit: 100.0}'

Starts `play_launch launch autosdv_rc_sim rc_sim.launch.xml rviz:=false` in its own
session (output in tmp/rc_sim_smoke_stack.log), waits until sim_pose_init has
initialized the localization, sets the route to the track's goal (the stop line),
waits until autonomous mode is available, engages, and watches /rc_sim/state.
PASS: the car exceeded 1.5 m/s, then came to rest (|v| < 0.05 m/s for 2 s of
simulated time) in the lane (|y| < 0.5 m) before the end of the run-out. Everything
is stopped by process group: SIGINT, SIGTERM, SIGKILL. Refuses ROS_DOMAIN_ID 0.
"""
import argparse
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import time

import yaml

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]


def stop_group(proc, label):
    if proc is None or proc.poll() is not None:
        return
    for sig, wait in ((signal.SIGINT, 15.0), (signal.SIGTERM, 5.0), (signal.SIGKILL, 5.0)):
        try:
            os.killpg(proc.pid, sig)
        except ProcessLookupError:
            return
        try:
            proc.wait(timeout=wait)
            return
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            print(f'{label}: still running after {sig.name}')


def drive(track, timeout, on_ready):
    import rclpy
    from autoware_adapi_v1_msgs.msg import LocalizationInitializationState, OperationModeState
    from autoware_adapi_v1_msgs.srv import ChangeOperationMode, SetRoutePoints
    from rclpy.node import Node
    from rclpy.qos import DurabilityPolicy, QoSProfile
    from std_msgs.msg import Float64MultiArray

    rclpy.init()
    node = Node('rc_sim_smoke')
    seen = {'loc': None, 'op': None, 'state': None}
    latched = QoSProfile(depth=1, durability=DurabilityPolicy.TRANSIENT_LOCAL)
    node.create_subscription(LocalizationInitializationState, '/api/localization/initialization_state',
                             lambda m: seen.__setitem__('loc', m.state), latched)
    node.create_subscription(OperationModeState, '/api/operation_mode/state',
                             lambda m: seen.__setitem__('op', m), latched)
    node.create_subscription(Float64MultiArray, '/rc_sim/state',
                             lambda m: seen.__setitem__('state', list(m.data)), 10)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout

    def spin_until(predicate, what):
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=0.2)
            if predicate():
                return True
        print(f'FAIL  timed out waiting for {what}')
        return False

    def call(client, request, what):
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if client.wait_for_service(timeout_sec=2.0):
                future = client.call_async(request)
                rclpy.spin_until_future_complete(node, future, timeout_sec=10.0)
                result = future.result()
                if result is not None and result.status.success:
                    return True
            time.sleep(2.0)
        print(f'FAIL  {what} never succeeded')
        return False

    try:
        if not spin_until(lambda: seen['loc'] == LocalizationInitializationState.INITIALIZED,
                          'the localization to be initialized (sim_pose_init)'):
            return 1
        on_ready()
        route = SetRoutePoints.Request()
        route.header.frame_id = 'map'
        goal = track['goal_pose']
        route.goal.position.x, route.goal.position.y = float(goal['x']), float(goal['y'])
        route.goal.orientation.z = math.sin(goal['yaw'] / 2.0)
        route.goal.orientation.w = math.cos(goal['yaw'] / 2.0)
        if not call(node.create_client(SetRoutePoints, '/api/routing/set_route_points'), route, 'set route'):
            return 1
        if not spin_until(lambda: seen['op'] is not None and seen['op'].is_autonomous_mode_available,
                          'autonomous mode to be available'):
            return 1
        if not call(node.create_client(ChangeOperationMode, '/api/operation_mode/change_to_autonomous'),
                    ChangeOperationMode.Request(), 'engage'):
            return 1
        vmax, still_since, last = 0.0, None, None
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=0.1)
            if seen['state'] is None:
                continue
            last = seen['state']
            t, vx = last[0], last[4]
            vmax = max(vmax, vx)
            if vmax > 1.5 and abs(vx) < 0.05:
                still_since = t if still_since is None else still_since
                if t - still_since >= 2.0:
                    break
            else:
                still_since = None
        if last is None:
            print('FAIL  no /rc_sim/state')
            return 1
        x, y = last[1], last[2]
        ok = vmax > 1.5 and still_since is not None and x <= track['length'] and abs(y) < 0.5
        print(f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}  vmax {vmax:.2f} m/s, at rest at x {x:.2f} m "
              f"(stop line {track['stop_line_s']:.2f}), y {y:+.2f} m")
        return 0 if ok else 1
    finally:
        node.destroy_node()
        rclpy.try_shutdown()


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument('--track', default=str(REPO / 'src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/tracks/straight_2_6_11'))
    ap.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=300.0, help='wall seconds for the whole run')
    ap.add_argument('--resources', default='', help='sample host resources into this CSV')
    ap.add_argument('--resource-seconds', type=float, default=120.0)
    ap.add_argument('--taskset', default='', help='pin the stack to these CPUs, e.g. 0-3')
    ap.add_argument('launch_args', nargs='*')
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if os.environ.setdefault('ROS_DOMAIN_ID', '73') == '0':
        print('refusing ROS_DOMAIN_ID 0 (shared host)')
        return 2
    track_dir = Path(args.track).resolve()
    track = yaml.safe_load((track_dir / 'track.yaml').read_text())
    cmd = ['play_launch', 'launch', '--web-addr', '127.0.0.1:8093', 'autosdv_rc_sim',
           'rc_sim.launch.xml', 'rviz:=false', f'track_dir:={track_dir}', *args.launch_args]
    if args.taskset:
        cmd = ['taskset', '-c', args.taskset, *cmd]
    (REPO / 'tmp').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    log = open(REPO / 'tmp/rc_sim_smoke_stack.log', 'w')
    stack = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=REPO, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
    sampler = None

    def start_sampler():
        nonlocal sampler
        if args.resources:
            sampler = subprocess.Popen(
                [sys.executable, str(REPO / 'scripts/profiling/host_resource_sampler.py'),
                 '-o', args.resources, '--pgid', str(os.getpgid(stack.pid)),
                 '--seconds', str(args.resource_seconds), '--label', 'rc_sim'],
                cwd=REPO, start_new_session=True)

    try:
        verdict = drive(track, args.timeout, start_sampler)
        if sampler is not None:
            sampler.wait()
        return verdict
    finally:
        stop_group(sampler, 'resource sampler')
        stop_group(stack, 'rc_sim stack')
        log.close()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

```just
#   just sim rc                                       the default straight, production coefficients
#   just sim rc submission:=~/lab2/B12345678_lab_2_1  a student's speed_controller_params.yaml
#   just sim rc track:=tmp/rc_sim/tracks/mine variant:=low_mu rviz:=false
# In RViz: 2D Goal Pose at the stop line, then Engage (or run scripts/testing/rc_sim/smoke.py).
# Not graded (the processes race within a tick); `just sim grade` is the grade.
# Interactive RC simulator: Autoware planning-simulator mode + the real vehicle interface + the plant
[positional-arguments]
rc *ARGS:
    #!/usr/bin/env bash
    source scripts/env.sh
    args=()
    for a in "$@"; do
        case "$a" in
            submission:=*)
                p=$(ros2 run autosdv_rc_sim rc_sim_grade "${a#submission:=}" --print-params) || exit 2
                args+=("speed_controller_params:=$p") ;;
            track:=*|map:=*) args+=("track_dir:=$(realpath "${a#*:=}")") ;;
            *) args+=("$a") ;;
        esac
    done
    [ -n "${DISPLAY:-}" ] || args+=(rviz:=false)
    cmd=(play_launch launch --web-addr '{{ web_addr }}' autosdv_rc_sim rc_sim.launch.xml "${args[@]}")
    printf '\033[2m%s\033[0m\n' "${cmd[*]}"
    exec "${cmd[@]}"
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Document.** Append to `docs/guides/rc-sim.md`:

````markdown
## Interactive mode (not graded)

```bash
just sim rc                                          # production coefficients
just sim rc submission:=~/lab2/<student_id>_lab_2_1  # yours
just sim rc track:=tmp/rc_sim/tracks/mine variant:=low_mu
```

Autoware runs in planning-simulator mode (map, planning, control, system,
API, RViz; no sensing, localization, perception or GPU). The car's vehicle
interface runs unchanged with `hardware:=sim`: the actuator's PWM goes to the
plant instead of the PCA9685, and `velocity_report` times the plant's hall
edges instead of a GPIO pin. The plant publishes `/clock`; every other node
uses simulated time. In RViz, put a 2D Goal Pose at the stop line and press
Engage. Speeds here come from Autoware's velocity planner, and separate
processes on a simulated clock race within a tick, so results are close to,
but not exactly, the graded run.

`just sim make-track --speeds 2,6,11 --hold 3 --accel 2.5 --decel 2.5` prints
the segment table and writes a track for `track:=`.
````

```python
"""Replay: picks the right row in time; publishes the trace's pose; marks violations.

Only the tests that need ROS skip without it (importorskip inside them); the violation
marks are plain Python and run everywhere.
"""
import os
import time

import pytest
import yaml

from autosdv_rc_sim import grade
from autosdv_rc_sim.lockstep import COLUMNS, Trace
from autosdv_rc_sim.track import default_track_dir, load_track


def test_index_at():
    pytest.importorskip('rclpy')
    from autosdv_rc_sim.replay_node import index_at
    times = [0.0, 0.01, 0.02, 0.03]
    assert [index_at(times, e) for e in (-1.0, 0.0, 0.015, 0.02, 9.0)] == [0, 0, 1, 2, 3]


def test_violations_are_placed_where_they_happened(tmp_path):
    profile = load_track(default_track_dir())
    report = {'variants': {'nominal': {'checks': {
        'hold_rmse_6': {'pass': False, 'value': 0.4, 'limit': 0.3},
        'stop_error': {'pass': False, 'value': 6.0, 'limit': 4.1},
        'launch_slip': {'pass': True, 'value': 0.1, 'limit': 0.5}}}}}
    n = grade.write_violations(report, profile, 'nominal', tmp_path / 'v.yaml')
    marks = yaml.safe_load((tmp_path / 'v.yaml').read_text())
    assert n == 2
    hold6 = [seg for seg in profile.segments if seg.kind == 'hold'][1]
    assert marks[0]['s'] == pytest.approx(hold6.s0) and 'hold_rmse_6' in marks[0]['text']
    assert marks[1]['s'] == pytest.approx(profile.stop_line_s)


def test_publishes_the_trace_pose(tmp_path):
    os.environ.setdefault('ROS_DOMAIN_ID', '73')
    rclpy = pytest.importorskip('rclpy')
    from nav_msgs.msg import Odometry
    from rclpy.executors import SingleThreadedExecutor
    from rclpy.parameter import Parameter
    from autosdv_rc_sim.replay_node import Replay

    rows = []
    for i in range(300):
        r = dict(t=0.01 * i, s=0.02 * i, y=0.01, yaw=0.0, target=2.0, measured=1.9, speed=2.0,
                 pwm=390, steer_pwm=489, mode='active', kappa_r=0.0, slip_r=0.0, ax=0.0,
                 delta=0.0, emergency=False, phase='run')
        rows.append(tuple(r[c] for c in COLUMNS))
    Trace('profile', 'nominal', rows).write_csv(tmp_path / 't.csv')
    rclpy.init()
    try:
        node = Replay(parameter_overrides=[Parameter('trace', value=str(tmp_path / 't.csv'))])
        probe = rclpy.create_node('replay_probe')
        got = []
        probe.create_subscription(Odometry, '/localization/kinematic_state',
                                  lambda m: got.append(m.pose.pose.position.x), 10)
        ex = SingleThreadedExecutor()
        ex.add_node(node)
        ex.add_node(probe)
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 5.0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline and (not got or got[-1] < 2.0):
            ex.spin_once(timeout_sec=0.01)
        assert got and got == sorted(got) and got[-1] > 2.0
        node.destroy_node()
        probe.destroy_node()
    finally:
        rclpy.try_shutdown()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `autosdv_rc_sim/replay_node.py`

```python
"""rc_sim_replay: play a graded trace on the topics Autoware's RViz reads.

What the student sees is exactly what was graded: the pose, speeds and PWM come
from the lockstep trace (100 Hz), replayed at `rate` x real time. Violations of
the grader's checks are marked on the map. No planner, no physics: cheap.
"""
from bisect import bisect_right

from autoware_internal_debug_msgs.msg import Float32MultiArrayStamped
from autoware_vehicle_msgs.msg import SteeringReport, VelocityReport
from geometry_msgs.msg import TransformStamped
from nav_msgs.msg import Odometry
import rclpy
from rclpy.executors import ExternalShutdownException
from rclpy.node import Node
from rclpy.qos import DurabilityPolicy, QoSProfile
import tf2_ros
from visualization_msgs.msg import Marker, MarkerArray
import yaml

from autosdv_rc_sim.detmath import cos, sin
from autosdv_rc_sim.lockstep import Trace


def index_at(times, elapsed: float) -> int:
    """The last row whose time is <= elapsed (clamped to the trace)."""
    return max(0, min(len(times) - 1, bisect_right(times, elapsed) - 1))


class Replay(Node):
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        super().__init__('rc_sim_replay', **kwargs)
        self.declare_parameter('trace', '')
        self.declare_parameter('violations', '')
        self.declare_parameter('rate', 1.0)
        self.declare_parameter('loop', False)
        self.trace = Trace.read_csv(self.get_parameter('trace').value)
        self.times = [t - self.trace.rows[0][0] for t in self.trace.column('t')]
        self.rate = float(self.get_parameter('rate').value)
        self.loop = bool(self.get_parameter('loop').value)
        self.cols = {name: self.trace.column(name) for name in
                     ('s', 'y', 'yaw', 'target', 'measured', 'speed', 'pwm', 'kappa_r', 'slip_r', 'delta')}
        self.pub_odom = self.create_publisher(Odometry, '/localization/kinematic_state', 10)
        self.pub_vel = self.create_publisher(VelocityReport, '/vehicle/status/velocity_status', 10)
        self.pub_steer = self.create_publisher(SteeringReport, '/vehicle/status/steering_status', 10)
        self.pub_values = self.create_publisher(Float32MultiArrayStamped, '/rc_sim/replay/values', 10)
        latched = QoSProfile(depth=1, durability=DurabilityPolicy.TRANSIENT_LOCAL)
        self.pub_marks = self.create_publisher(MarkerArray, '/rc_sim/replay/violations', latched)
        self.tf = tf2_ros.TransformBroadcaster(self)
        self.start = self.get_clock().now()
        self.publish_violations(self.get_parameter('violations').value)
        self.create_timer(0.01, self.tick)

    def publish_violations(self, path: str) -> None:
        if not path:
            return
        with open(path) as f:
            marks = yaml.safe_load(f) or []
        array = MarkerArray()
        for i, m in enumerate(marks):
            for kind, offset in ((Marker.SPHERE, 0.0), (Marker.TEXT_VIEW_FACING, 0.6)):
                mk = Marker()
                mk.header.frame_id = 'map'
                mk.ns, mk.id, mk.type = 'rc_sim_violations', 2 * i + (kind == Marker.TEXT_VIEW_FACING), kind
                mk.pose.position.x, mk.pose.position.z = float(m['s']), offset
                mk.pose.orientation.w = 1.0
                mk.scale.x = mk.scale.y = mk.scale.z = 0.3
                mk.color.r, mk.color.a = 1.0, 1.0
                mk.text = m['text']
                array.markers.append(mk)
        self.pub_marks.publish(array)

    def tick(self) -> None:
        now = self.get_clock().now()
        elapsed = (now - self.start).nanoseconds * 1e-9 * self.rate
        if self.loop and elapsed > self.times[-1]:
            self.start = now
            elapsed = 0.0
        i = index_at(self.times, elapsed)
        c = self.cols
        stamp = now.to_msg()
        qz, qw = sin(0.5 * c['yaw'][i]), cos(0.5 * c['yaw'][i])
        tf = TransformStamped()
        tf.header.stamp, tf.header.frame_id, tf.child_frame_id = stamp, 'map', 'base_link'
        tf.transform.translation.x, tf.transform.translation.y = c['s'][i], c['y'][i]
        tf.transform.rotation.z, tf.transform.rotation.w = qz, qw
        self.tf.sendTransform(tf)
        odom = Odometry()
        odom.header.stamp, odom.header.frame_id, odom.child_frame_id = stamp, 'map', 'base_link'
        odom.pose.pose.position.x, odom.pose.pose.position.y = c['s'][i], c['y'][i]
        odom.pose.pose.orientation.z, odom.pose.pose.orientation.w = qz, qw
        odom.twist.twist.linear.x = c['speed'][i]
        self.pub_odom.publish(odom)
        vel = VelocityReport()
        vel.header.stamp, vel.header.frame_id = stamp, 'base_link'
        vel.longitudinal_velocity = c['measured'][i]
        self.pub_vel.publish(vel)
        steer = SteeringReport()
        steer.stamp, steer.steering_tire_angle = stamp, c['delta'][i]
        self.pub_steer.publish(steer)
        values = Float32MultiArrayStamped()
        values.stamp = stamp
        values.data = [float(c[k][i]) for k in ('target', 'measured', 'speed', 'pwm', 'kappa_r', 'slip_r')]
        self.pub_values.publish(values)


def main(args=None):
    rclpy.init(args=args)
    node = None
    try:
        node = Replay()
        rclpy.spin(node)
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, ExternalShutdownException):
        pass
    finally:
        if node is not None:
            node.destroy_node()
        rclpy.try_shutdown()
```

Add to `grade.py` (imports: `import signal`, `import subprocess`, and `EstopScenario` is already imported):

```python
def write_violations(report: dict, profile: TrackProfile, variant: str, path) -> int:
    """Where on the straight each failed check of `variant` happened, for the replay."""
    holds = {f'{seg.v0:g}': seg.s0 for seg in profile.segments if seg.kind == 'hold'}
    marks = []
    for name, c in report['variants'][variant]['checks'].items():
        if c['pass']:
            continue
        head, _, speed = name.rpartition('_')
        if head in ('hold_rmse', 'overshoot', 'reach'):
            s = holds[speed]
        elif name.startswith('launch_'):
            s = 0.5
        elif name in ('stop_error', 'brake_lock'):
            s = profile.stop_line_s
        else:
            s = EstopScenario(profile).trigger_s
        value = 'none' if c['value'] is None else f"{c['value']:.3g}"
        marks.append({'s': float(s), 'text': f"{name}: {value} (limit {c['limit']:.3g})"})
    Path(path).write_text(yaml.safe_dump(marks, sort_keys=False))
    return len(marks)


def view(trace_csv, track_dir, violations) -> int:
    """Replay a trace in Autoware's RViz until Ctrl-C; stops the stack by process group."""
    cmd = ['play_launch', 'launch', '--web-addr', os.environ.get('RC_SIM_WEB_ADDR', '127.0.0.1:8094'),
           'autosdv_rc_sim', 'rc_sim_replay.launch.xml', f'trace:={Path(trace_csv).resolve()}',
           f'track_dir:={Path(track_dir).resolve()}', f'violations:={Path(violations).resolve()}']
    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, start_new_session=True)
    try:
        return proc.wait()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGKILL):
            try:
                os.killpg(proc.pid, sig)
                proc.wait(timeout=10.0)
                break
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                continue
            except ProcessLookupError:
                break
        return 0
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The launch file** — `launch/rc_sim_replay.launch.xml`

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!--
  Replay a graded rc_sim run in Autoware's RViz (`just sim grade DIR --view`): the
  track's map, the vehicle model, and the trace on the topics RViz reads. No
  planning, control or physics.
-->
<launch>
  <arg name="trace" description="a trace CSV written by rc_sim_grade (traces/<variant>_<scenario>.csv)"/>
  <arg name="violations" default="" description="violations_<variant>.yaml written by rc_sim_grade"/>
  <arg name="track_dir" default="$(find-pkg-share autosdv_rc_sim)/tracks/straight_2_6_11"/>
  <arg name="rate" default="1.0"/>
  <arg name="loop" default="false"/>
  <arg name="vehicle_model" default="autosdv_vehicle"/>
  <arg name="sensor_model" default="autosdv_sensor_kit"/>

  <include file="$(find-pkg-share autoware_launch)/launch/autoware.launch.xml">
    <arg name="map_path" value="$(var track_dir)"/>
    <arg name="vehicle_model" value="$(var vehicle_model)"/>
    <arg name="sensor_model" value="$(var sensor_model)"/>
    <arg name="launch_vehicle" value="true"/>
    <arg name="launch_vehicle_interface" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_map" value="true"/>
    <arg name="launch_system" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_sensing" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_localization" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_perception" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_planning" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_control" value="false"/>
    <arg name="launch_api" value="false"/>
    <arg name="is_simulation" value="true"/>
    <arg name="rviz" value="true"/>
  </include>

  <node pkg="autosdv_rc_sim" exec="rc_sim_replay" name="rc_sim_replay" output="screen">
    <param name="trace" value="$(var trace)"/>
    <param name="violations" value="$(var violations)"/>
    <param name="rate" value="$(var rate)"/>
    <param name="loop" value="$(var loop)"/>
  </node>
</launch>
```

## Task 20: Measure the laptop budget

The spec targets a 4-core, 8 GB laptop with no GPU: under 3 cores and 4 GB for the full stack without RViz.

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/reports/host-resource-measurements.md`, `docs/guides/rc-sim.md`
- Modify (only if the budget is missed): `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/launch/rc_sim.launch.xml`, `plant_node.py` defaults

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `smoke.py --resources --taskset` (Task 18), `scripts/profiling/host_resource_sampler.py`.
- Produces: a measured row in the resource report.

- [ ] **Step 1: Measure on four CPUs, without RViz**

```bash
cd /home/aeon/repos/AutoSDV && source install/setup.bash
ROS_DOMAIN_ID=73 flock tmp/bench.lock python3 scripts/testing/rc_sim/smoke.py \
  --taskset 0-3 --resources tmp/rc_sim_budget.csv --resource-seconds 120 \
  "speed_controller_params:={max_pwm_offset: 100, integral_limit: 100.0}"
```
Expected: `PASS …`, followed by the sampler's summary: peak memory over baseline and CPU in whole cores for the stack's process group.

- [ ] **Step 2: Check against the budget.** Pass: steady CPU < 3.0 cores and peak RAM < 4.0 GiB. Also check the plant itself, `top -b -n 5 -d 2 -p "$(pgrep -f rc_sim_plant)"`: it should be under 0.2 core (spec). If the budget is missed, apply these levers in order and re-measure after each:
  1. `clock_period` 0.005 → 0.01 (halves `/clock` traffic to every node).

## Task 21: Calibration hooks `fit.py` (`just sim fit-coastdown`)

So that the guessed parameters can be refitted once the car drives: a coast-down fit for rolling resistance and drag, and the skidpad relation for μ. The motor fit already exists (`just coach fit-motor`).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/autosdv_rc_sim/fit.py`
- Create: `src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim/test/test_fit.py`
- Modify: `just/sim.just`, `docs/guides/rc-sim.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `load_vehicle`, `road_load` (test only), `GRAVITY`.
- Produces:
  - `fit_coastdown(t, v, effective_mass) -> (a0, a2)`: least squares on m_eff·(−dv/dt) = a0 + a2·v².
  - `skidpad_mu(radius, speed) -> float`.
  - CLI `rc_sim_fit coastdown CSV [--variant nominal]`, where the CSV has columns `t,v` from a coast-down at neutral on the flat. It prints a0, a2 and the implied `rolling_resistance` (a0 less the drivetrain Coulomb term, divided by m·g) and `aero_drag` (≈ a2).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** — `test/test_fit.py`

```python
"""Coast-down fit recovers the model's road load; the skidpad relation."""
from dataclasses import replace

import pytest

from autosdv_rc_sim.drivetrain import road_load
from autosdv_rc_sim.fit import fit_coastdown, skidpad_mu
from autosdv_rc_sim.params import load_vehicle
from autosdv_rc_sim.plant import Plant


def test_coastdown_fit_recovers_the_road_load():
    p = load_vehicle()
    plant = Plant(p)
    w = 9.0 / p.wheel_radius
    plant.state = replace(plant.state, vx=9.0, omega_f=w, omega_r=w)
    t, v = [], []
    for k in range(8000):
        plant.step(p.physics_dt, 370, 489)
        if k % 10 == 0 and plant.state.vx > 1.0:
            t.append(k * p.physics_dt)
            v.append(plant.state.vx)
    a0, a2 = fit_coastdown(t, v, p.effective_mass)
    for speed in (2.0, 6.0, 9.0):
        assert a0 + a2 * speed * speed == pytest.approx(road_load(p, speed), rel=0.05)


def test_skidpad_mu():
    assert skidpad_mu(2.0, 4.0) == pytest.approx(16.0 / (9.81 * 2.0))
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run, expect FAIL**

```bash
cd /home/aeon/repos/AutoSDV/src/sim/autosdv_rc_sim && python3 -m pytest test/test_fit.py -q
```
Expected: FAIL. `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'autosdv_rc_sim.fit'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `autosdv_rc_sim/fit.py`

```python
"""Calibration hooks: refit the guessed resistances and friction from car data.

    rc_sim_fit coastdown run.csv           columns t,v: a coast-down at neutral on the flat

Coast-down: m_eff (-dv/dt) = a0 + a2 v^2, least squares on central-difference
decelerations. a0 is rolling resistance plus the drivetrain's Coulomb drag at
the ground; a2 is the aerodynamic coefficient (the small viscous term folds
into both). Skidpad: on a circle of radius R at the speed where the car starts
to slide, mu = v^2 / (g R). The motor and gear: `just coach fit-motor`.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import sys
from typing import Sequence, Tuple

from autosdv_rc_sim.params import GRAVITY, load_vehicle


def fit_coastdown(t: Sequence[float], v: Sequence[float], effective_mass: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
    xs, ys = [], []
    for i in range(1, len(t) - 1):
        decel = -(v[i + 1] - v[i - 1]) / (t[i + 1] - t[i - 1])
        xs.append(v[i] * v[i])
        ys.append(effective_mass * decel)
    n = len(xs)
    sx, sy = sum(xs), sum(ys)
    sxx = sum(x * x for x in xs)
    sxy = sum(x * y for x, y in zip(xs, ys))
    a2 = (n * sxy - sx * sy) / (n * sxx - sx * sx)
    a0 = (sy - a2 * sx) / n
    return a0, a2


def skidpad_mu(radius: float, speed: float) -> float:
    return speed * speed / (GRAVITY * radius)


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Refit rc_sim parameters from car data.')
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest='cmd', required=True)
    cd = sub.add_parser('coastdown', help='CSV with columns t,v')
    cd.add_argument('csv')
    cd.add_argument('--variant', default='nominal')
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    p = load_vehicle(variant=args.variant)
    with open(args.csv) as f:
        rows = [(float(r['t']), float(r['v'])) for r in csv.DictReader(f)]
    a0, a2 = fit_coastdown([r[0] for r in rows], [r[1] for r in rows], p.effective_mass)
    coulomb = (p.drivetrain_coulomb + p.front_bearing_coulomb) / p.wheel_radius
    print(f'a0 = {a0:.3f} N, a2 = {a2:.4f} N/(m/s)^2')
    print(f'rolling_resistance ~ {(a0 - coulomb) / (p.mass * GRAVITY):.4f} '
          f'(model {p.rolling_resistance}); aero_drag ~ {a2:.4f} (model {p.aero_drag})')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run, expect PASS** (same command). Expected: 2 passed.

```just
[positional-arguments]
fit-coastdown CSV *ARGS:
    #!/usr/bin/env bash
    source scripts/env.sh
    exec ros2 run autosdv_rc_sim rc_sim_fit coastdown "$@"
```

and append a "Calibrating the model against the car" section to `docs/guides/rc-sim.md` listing the measurements, in the order the `vehicle.yaml` notes give them: weigh the car (`mass`); find its balance point (`lf`); do a coast-down (`just sim fit-coastdown`) for `rolling_resistance` and `aero_drag`; run a skidpad or tilt test for `mu` (`fit.skidpad_mu`); fit the motor (`just coach fit-motor`) to replace the **mock** `motor_kv`/`gear_ratio`. ## Task 22: Course handout and grader migration (separate repository — task list only)

Part 1 grading moves to this simulator. The course repository (`~/repos/ntu-sdv-course`, `2026/labs/Lab2/`) is changed in its own plan, written against the AutoSDV commit that finishes Task 21. This repository stays self-contained: nothing in Tasks 1–21 reads the course repository. The stub, for that plan:

- [ ] Handout: replace Part 1's offline benches (`handout/lab2_waypoint/lab2_waypoint/bench/`: `plant_model.py`, `speed_bench.py`, `speed_eval.py`, `run_metrics.py`, `params.py`, `vendor/`, `models/`) with a `speed_controller_params.yaml` template (production values and comments) and a README section pointing at `just sim grade`, `just sim grade --view` and `docs/guides/rc-sim.md`.
- [ ] Remove per-car Part 1 material the spec retires: the car CSVs, `config/calibration.yaml`, `calibration.py`, `ta/calibration_check.py`, and the feed-forward (`ff_deadband`, `ff_gain`) path. Update `selfcheck.py` (the rubric: `CALIBRATION_POINTS` is gone).
- [ ] `ta/grade.py`: compute Part 1's automatic marks by running `python3 -m autosdv_rc_sim.grade <submission> --jobs N --out <dir> --points 15` (or `ros2 run autosdv_rc_sim rc_sim_grade`) and reading `marks.json`. Refuse submissions whose file holds keys outside `ALLOWED_KEYS` (the grader already does; surface its message). Pin the AutoSDV commit in `ta/lab2-ta-grading.md`, since the marks depend on the model.
- [ ] `ta/reference/`: replace the reference SpeedPID with AutoSDV's `config/reference_speed_controller.yaml`. `ta/grade.py --reference` must score the full 15: Task 16's test requires the reference to pass all 16 checks on every variant, each at most 90 % of its limit, so a lower score means the checkout's model differs from the one the limits were calibrated on.
- [ ] `ta/fuzz/`: submissions with unknown keys, out-of-range values, wrong types, and a missing file must each be refused with exit 2.
- [ ] Report questions: the textbook PID → `AckermannSpeedPID` mapping (sampled integral, derivative on the measurement with its filter, output clamp, integral limit, conditional integration); why the production coefficients cannot hold 11 m/s; why `pwm_output_alpha` changes nothing; what the rear-only brake does to the stop.
- [ ] Lab spec (TeX): Part 1 becomes simulation-only; the profile table (0 → 2 → 6 → 11 m/s, 115.4 m); the variants; the checks; the deliverable (one YAML plus a report).
- [ ] `lab2_config.py` / `submission.py` in AutoSDV's `scripts/testing/lab2/`: Part 2 still loads a team's `speed_pid.py` controller. Decide with the course staff whether Part 2 keeps the student's own SpeedPID or switches to `AckermannSpeedPID` with the Part 1 coefficients. That decision changes `submission.py`, so it gets its own AutoSDV task.

The procedure that produces each number is given in full, and Task 16 states the values this model gave (`overshoot_pct` 11, `launch_accel` 4.8, reference kp 7, ki 43, kd 1.5), which the deterministic lockstep reproduces.

- Topic names: `/rc_sim/pwm` and `/rc_sim/hall_edges` are defined once, as `PWM_TOPIC` and `EDGE_TOPIC` in `hal.py`; `plant_node.py` and its test import them rather than repeat the strings.

**Review Focus coverage:** each item has its pinning test in its owning task: 1 → Tasks 7 and 13; 2 → Task 4; 3 → Tasks 11 and 15; 4 → Tasks 6 and 17; 5 → Tasks 5, 10 and 14; 6 → Tasks 8 and 11.

**Decisions for the user, raised by this plan (Spec Findings), and how they were settled:**
1. d = 3.5 m/s² is beyond what rear-only braking gives. Decided: d = 2.5 m/s². The straight is 115.4 m (stop line 105.4 m, about 17.8 s), every variant can follow the braking ramp, and the stop-error limit is a plain 0.5 m.
2. `pwm_output_alpha` has no effect in today's actuator. It stays in the key list and the guide says so; making it work is a production change.
3. The loaded top speed is ≈ 12.2 m/s rather than 11. This keeps the 11 m/s hold regulable.
4. Model constants the plan had to move to make the checks passable, all tagged `guess` and to be refitted from car data: rolling resistance 0.02 → 0.05 and front bearing friction 0.002 → 0.02 N·m (more road load for the braking ramp), ESC current limits 120 A motoring and 8.65 A regenerating (new), and the variants `heavy` 3.6 kg, `light` 3.3 kg, `low_mu` 0.75 and `deadband_wide` 7 (from 4.0, 3.0, 0.7 and 8, which made the lock or resume checks unpassable for the model's reasons). The calibrated limits come out at `overshoot_pct` 11 % and `launch_accel` 4.8 m/s², and the reference (kp 7, ki 43, kd 1.5, integral limit 100) leaves about 15 % of margin on the stop error and the resume slip, no more.
